Can an attacker who controls the archive still pass this if they ALSO forge the live store — i.e. does verify_checkpoint actually prevent that (does the signed head cover the archived seqs' entry_hashes)? Any deadlock/re-entrancy with a concurrent checkpoint (which also takes floor_lock on a DIFFERENT path)? The codec-invariant idempotency: could two DIFFERENT segments (or a genuinely-corrupted re-archive) now pass the identity check and be wrongly treated as idempotent (silently keeping stale bytes)?\n6. {"parentUuid":"1ce769ac-7420-4c94-aebf-3d8535a25d45","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"<command-message>crucible</command-message>\n<command-name>crucible</command-name>\n<skill-format>true</skill-format>"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main/.claude/skills/crucible\n\n# CRUCIBLE — operating the engine\n\nCRUCIBLE turns an authorized seed URL into **oracle-confirmed** findings, reasons over them,\nand produces auditable reports — inside a fail-closed safety stack. Its whole value is one\nrule; internalize it before anything else:\n\n> **A claim is a FACT only when a deterministic oracle fires over data a real target\n> produced.** You (the LLM) ADVISE where to look and what a result might mean; the oracle\n> CONFIRMS. Nothing else — not your confidence, not a critic's endorsement, not a plausible\n> story — promotes a claim to a fact.\n\nThe standing doctrine every reasoning call operates under is\n[framework/cognitive/metacognition.md](../../../framework/cognitive/metacognition.md) (it is\ninjected into every LLM system prompt). The full architecture — agents, event spine, veracity\nlayer, learning/metacognition core, safety stack — is\n[framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md](../../../framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md). The binding\nconstitution is [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md); never relax scope, destruction, evidence, or\nhonesty rules.\n\n## The invariants (never violate these)\n\n1. **Oracle authority.** Only a fired oracle confirms. Critics/RL/reflection/self-consistency\n   may only *advise, re-rank, defer, or abstain* — never promote a finding or override the oracle.\n2. **Coverage.** Never silently skip an authorized attack surface. Learning and reflection\n   *deprioritise* (re-rank / defer); they never gate a surface out.\n3. **Prove by re-execution.** A finding is a fact only if its retained `oracle_context`\n   re-fires. The veracity firewall (`framework/v2/veracity/`) re-runs proofs; it can only demote.\n4. **Non-circular learning.** An EXPLOITABLE label needs ≥2 independent corroborating oracle\n   kinds; a silent oracle is never auto-labelled a false positive.\n5. **Refuse honestly, fail closed.** Decline to conclude what you cannot ground; record every\n   refusal as evidence. Hard limits (scope, authorization, destruction, real user data) are\n   inviolable — when unsure, stop and ask.\n6. **Determinism + append-only.** No wallclock/global-rng in learning/reward math; the event\n   spine is append-only (supersede, never edit).\n\n## Structured workflows\n\n### Run an authorized engagement (end-to-end, gated)\n1. Confirm the active target's `targets/<slug>/charter.md` authorizes it (constitution §II).\n2. `python3 -m framework.v2 engage <slug> <seed-url> [--recon] [--spine]`\n   — crawls, audits, confirms via oracles, chains attack paths, scores confidence, and\n   (with `--spine`) mirrors the whole run onto the immutable blackboard event stream.\n   (`--strict-evidence` is a **`scan`**-only flag, below — not an `engage` flag.)\n3. Every request passes the full gate chain; an out-of-scope seed or tripped kill-switch\n   refuses *before* traffic and is recorded as a `refusal` event.\n\n### Loopback-only quick scan\n`python3 -m framework.v2 scan http://127.0.0.1:<port>/ --format json|sarif|html [--strict-evidence]`\n— the export states each finding's **live grounding** (fact / ungrounded / contradicted);\n`--strict-evidence` withholds non-fact findings from the rendered doc but keeps them in\n`--reverifiable-out`.\n\n### Re-verify a report (prove-don't-guess, offline)\n`python3 -m framework.v2 verify <report.json>` re-runs each finding's retained `oracle_context`.\n`python3 -m framework.v2 evidence ...` builds/verifies signed, hash-linked evidence certificates\n(and, with report claims bound in, fails closed when a report sentence has no backing evidence).\n\n### Inspect / audit the event spine\nThe blackboard (`framework/v2/agents/blackboard.py`) is the one append-only, typed, provenance-\nlinked event stream. Replay it with `Blackboard.replay(engagement=..., since_id=...)`.\nCryptographic tamper-evidence: `framework/v2/agents/spine_chain.py`\n(`build_spine_chain` / `sign_spine_head` / `verify_spine_head`).\n\n### Recon / intel, memory, self-improvement\n`python3 -m framework.v2 intel ...` (OSINT into the shared world-model; gated egress),\n`memory ...` (cross-engagement priors — never fabricate a score), `improve ...`\n(authorise-not-apply — it never self-applies a change).\n\n### Read-only operator console\n`python3 -m framework.v2 console` — loopback, read-only, zero-impact operator UI over the run.\n\n## What NOT to do\n- Do not build detection-evasion / identity-rotation / stay-hidden-from-defenders capability —\n  CRUCIBLE is correlatable and authorized by doctrine (constitution §VI).\n- Do not let any learned/critic/LLM signal enter the deterministic oracle/SCE/calibration inputs\n  or promote a finding.\n- Do not attack third parties (payment/IdP/CDN) — test only the operator's integration with them.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"uuid":"cd0dac78-9942-4650-9c9f-459c50bff9ac","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:39:51.248Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

NOTHING here deletes a live record or commits a head — Slice E wires\n4\tthese into the crash-safe cutover. Everything is testable on a throwaway spine.\n5\t\n6\tThe archive is `SIGIL_HOME/spine/archive/` (relocatable via `SIGIL_SPINE_ARCHIVE_DIR`):\n7\t    archive/segments/seg-*.jsonl[.gz]     # whole sealed segments, byte-identical (copy, never a rename)\n8\t    archive/archive.manifest.json         # APPEND-ONLY across prunes: one row per archived segment + snapshot\n9\t\n10\tDoctrine: MOVE = copy → verify → (Slice E) drop; here we stop after copy+verify (additive-only). The\n11\tverifier (`verify_with_archive`) anchors the archive to the OWNER SIGNATURE — never to the (attacker-\n12\tcontrollable) archive manifest: it requires a valid owner-signed live head, checks the per-record content\n13\tbinding + the genesis chain, and ties the archived leaves to the owner key (before a prune commits: the\n14\tarchive must equal the still-present live prefix; after: the archive tail == the signed head.base_prev_hash\n15\tand the re-derived cumulative == the signed head.cumulative_merkle_root). Only then is the manifest's own\n16\tMerkle re-derivation a meaningful self-consistency cross-check. Losing the archive makes the pruned prefix\n17\tunrecoverable (only the owner-signed Merkle commitment survives) — an explicit MAX-RECLAIM tradeoff.\n18\t\"\"\"\n19\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n20\t\n21\timport gzip\n22\timport hashlib\n23\timport json\n24\timport os\n25\tfrom pathlib import Path\n26\tfrom typing import Any, Optional\n27\t\n28\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME, SCOPE\n29\tfrom ..reuse.chain import _GENESIS_PREV, _entry_hash, verify_chain\n30\tfrom ..reuse.models import ChainEntry\n31\tfrom .atomicio import atomic_write_text, fsync_dir\n32\tfrom .manifest import Segment, read_manifest\n33\tfrom .merkle import chain_cumulative, merkle_root\n34\tfrom .models import SpineRecord\n35\tfrom .snapshot import SnapshotState, build\n36\t\n37\t\n38\tclass PruneUnsafe(Exception):\n39\t    \"\"\"A prune point K fails a referential-integrity guard (§7) — refuse; touch nothing.\"\"\"\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tdef archive_dir() -> Path:\n43\t    env = os.environ.get(\"SIGIL_SPINE_ARCHIVE_DIR\")\n44\t    return Path(env) if env else (SIGIL_HOME / \"spine\" / \"archive\")\n45\t\n46\t\n47\t# ---- segment IO (gz-aware, standalone so the archive path never depends on a live store handle) ---------\n48\tdef _open_seg(path: Path):\n49\t    return gzip.open(path, \"rt\", encoding=\"utf-8\") if str(path).endswith(\".gz\") else open(path, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n50\t\n51\t\n52\tdef read_segment_records(path: Path) -> list[SpineRecord]:\n53\t    out: list[SpineRecord] = []\n54\t    with _open_seg(path) as f:\n55\t        for raw in f:\n56\t            if raw.strip():\n57\t                out.append(SpineRecord.from_dict(json.loads(raw)))\n58\t    return out\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef _sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:\n62\t    h = hashlib.sha256()\n63\t    with open(path, \"rb\") as f:                             # hash the ON-DISK bytes (gz stays gz — byte-identical)\n64\t        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(65536), b\"\"):\n65\t            h.update(chunk)\n66\t    return h.hexdigest()\n67\t\n68\t\n69\t# ---- §7 referential-integrity guards -------------------------------------------------------------------\n70\tdef open_workflow_floor(store) -> int:\n71\t    \"\"\"§7(b): the lowest seq that ANY still-open workflow — or anything it references — sits at. A prune\n72\t    boundary K must be <= this floor so no open loop (or its cited history) is ever pruned. Returns the\n73\t    live tip's next_seq when nothing is open (any segment-aligned K is then referentially safe). CONSERVATIVE\n74\t    by construction: a source we cannot enumerate can only make the true floor LOWER, so callers must treat\n75\t    this as an UPPER bound and the review must confirm completeness before Slice E enables deletion.\"\"\"\n76\t    from ..agents.approvals import pending\n77\t    from ..consolidate.queries import due_commitments, open_threads, pending_contradictions\n78\t\n79\t    floor = store.next_seq                                  # nothing open -> the tip\n80\t    seqs: list[int] = []\n81\t    for item in pending(store):                             # unresolved approval proposals + their targets\n82\t        p = item.payload\n83\t        seqs.append(item.seq)\n84\t        for k in (\"target_seq\", \"proposal_seq\", \"plan_seq\", \"step_seq\"):\n85\t            v = p.get(k)\n86\t            if isinstance(v, int):\n87\t                seqs.append(v)\n88\t    for row in open_threads(store, limit=10_000):           # open threads + cited source_seqs\n89\t        seqs.append(row[\"seq\"])\n90\t        seqs.extend(s for s in (row.get(\"source_seqs\") or []) if isinstance(s, int))\n91\t    for row in due_commitments(store, limit=10_000):\n92\t        seqs.append(row[\"seq\"])\n93\t        seqs.extend(s for s in (row.get(\"source_seqs\") or []) if isinstance(s, int))\n94\t    for row in pending_contradictions(store, limit=10_000):\n95\t        seqs.append(row[\"seq\"])\n96\t        seqs.extend(s for s in (row.get(\"source_seqs\") or []) if isinstance(s, int))\n97\t    # un-executed OPERATOR plans: a `operator.plan` not yet resolved by a terminal `operator.execute`\n98\t    # (APPLIED or FAILED+ROLLED_BACK) or a `refused` — its plan + preview must stay live until executed.\n99\t    plans: dict[int, int] = {}                              # plan_seq -> its own seq\n100\t    resolved: set[int] = set()\n101\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n102\t        p = r.payload\n103\t        sig = p.get(\"signal\")\n104\t        if sig == \"operator.plan\":\n105\t            plans[r.seq] = r.seq\n106\t        elif sig == \"operator.execute\" and isinstance(p.get(\"target_seq\"), int):\n107\t            resolved.add(p[\"target_seq\"])                   # any execute (APPLIED or FAILED+ROLLED_BACK) closes it\n108\t        elif p.get(\"decision\") == \"refused\" and isinstance(p.get(\"target_seq\"), int):\n109\t            resolved.add(p[\"target_seq\"])\n110\t    for plan_seq in plans:\n111\t        if plan_seq not in resolved:\n112\t            seqs.append(plan_seq)                           # open plan — nothing below it may be pruned\n113\t    if seqs:\n114\t        floor = min(floor, min(seqs))\n115\t    return floor\n116\t\n117\t\n118\tdef check_prune_safe(store, K: int) -> list[Segment]:\n119\t    \"\"\"Validate a prune boundary K and return the sealed segments to archive (whole segments, last_seq < K),\n120\t    oldest-first. Raises PruneUnsafe on any violation. Reads only; touches nothing.\"\"\"\n121\t    m = read_manifest(store._layout)\n122\t    if m is None:\n123\t        raise PruneUnsafe(\"legacy (un-migrated) spine — run `sigil spine migrate` before pruning\")\n124\t    sealed = m.sealed_in_order()\n125\t    # (d) K MUST be a sealed-segment first_seq (whole segments only; base_prev_hash is then a stored value).\n126\t    boundaries = {s.first_seq for s in sealed}\n127\t    if K not in boundaries:\n128\t        raise PruneUnsafe(f\"K={K} is not a sealed-segment boundary (aligns to whole segments only); \"\n129\t                          f\"valid boundaries: {sorted(boundaries)}\")\n130\t    if K <= 0:\n131\t        raise PruneUnsafe(\"K must be > 0 (nothing to prune at genesis)\")\n132\t    # (b) referential floor: every open workflow + everything it cites stays live.\n133\t    floor = open_workflow_floor(store)\n134\t    if K > floor:\n135\t        raise PruneUnsafe(f\"K={K} exceeds the open-workflow referential floor {floor} — an open loop or a \"\n136\t                          f\"record it cites would be pruned; lower K or resolve the open work first\")\n137\t    archived = [s for s in sealed if s.last_seq is not None and s.last_seq < K]\n138\t    if not archived:\n139\t        raise PruneUnsafe(f\"no whole sealed segment lies entirely below K={K} — nothing to archive\")\n140\t    # (a) dangling OPEN reference: a retained record whose parent/supersedes points below K into a seq that\n141\t    # is NOT itself covered by the archive (i.e. would become unresolvable). A closed ref is benign\n142\t    # (resolve()->PrunedRef in Slice E); an OPEN one already blocked by the floor above. Here assert the\n143\t    # archived set is a contiguous genesis-rooted prefix so no retained record links to a HOLE.\n144\t    if archived[0].first_seq != 0:\n145\t        raise PruneUnsafe(\"archive set does not start at genesis seq 0 — a non-prefix prune would strand refs\")\n146\t    exp = 0\n147\t    for s in archived:\n148\t        if s.first_seq != exp:\n149\t            raise PruneUnsafe(f\"archive set is non-contiguous at seg {s.id} (expected first_seq {exp})\")\n150\t        exp = (s.last_seq or 0) + 1\n151\t    if exp != K:\n152\t        raise PruneUnsafe(f\"archive set covers [0..{exp}) but K={K} — must be exactly contiguous to K\")\n153\t    return archived\n154\t\n155\t\n156\t# ---- the owner-committed snapshot payload (Merkle accumulator + folded state) ---------------------------\n157\tdef snapshot_payload(store, K: int, *, prior: Optional[dict] = None,\n158\t                     trusted_pubkey: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n159\t    \"\"\"Compute the `kind=\"snapshot\"` record payload committing the pruned prefix [0..K). `prior` is the\n160\t    previous snapshot's payload dict (None for the first prune). Pure computation — appends nothing.\"\"\"\n161\t    from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n162\t    tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else (owner_pubkey() or \"\")\n163\t    archived = check_prune_safe(store, K)\n164\t    k_prev = int(prior[\"base_seq\"]) if prior else 0\n165\t    prior_cumulative = str(prior[\"cumulative_merkle_root\"]) if prior else \"\"\n166\t    # the prior snapshot's OWN seq is only known once it has been appended (Slice E stamps it back into the\n167\t    # payload); a not-yet-committed prior (e.g. the fold-of-fold computation) carries none -> -1.\n168\t    prior_snapshot_seq = int(prior.get(\"snapshot_seq\", -1)) if prior else -1\n169\t    prior_folded = SnapshotState.model_validate(prior[\"folded_state\"]) if prior else None\n170\t\n171\t    # delta = the records pruned THIS round: [k_prev .. K-1], read from the segments being archived now.\n172\t    delta: list[SpineRecord] = []\n173\t    for seg in archived:\n174\t        if seg.first_seq >= k_prev:                         # a segment newly archived this round\n175\t            delta.extend(read_segment_records(store._layout.seg_path(seg)))\n176\t    delta.sort(key=lambda r: r.seq)\n177\t    if delta:\n178\t        if delta[0].seq != k_prev or delta[-1].seq != K - 1 or len(delta) != K - k_prev:\n179\t            raise PruneUnsafe(f\"delta records do not cover [{k_prev}..{K}) exactly (got \"\n180\t                              f\"{delta[0].seq}..{delta[-1].seq}, n={len(delta)})\")\n181\t\n182\t    delta_root = merkle_root([r.entry_hash for r in delta])\n183\t    cumulative = chain_cumulative(prior_cumulative, delta_root)\n184\t    base_prev_hash = archived[-1].boundary_hash or _GENESIS_PREV   # == entry_hash(K-1), a stored boundary\n185\t    folded = build(delta, trusted_pubkey=tp, base_seq=K, snapshot_seq=-1, seed=prior_folded)\n186\t    return {\n187\t        \"signal\": \"spine.snapshot\",\n188\t        \"base_seq\": K,\n189\t        \"base_prev_hash\": base_prev_hash,\n190\t        \"base_count\": K,                                    # contiguous from genesis -> == K\n191\t        \"delta_merkle_root\": delta_root,\n192\t        \"cumulative_merkle_root\": cumulative,\n193\t        \"prev_snapshot_seq\": prior_snapshot_seq,\n194\t        \"trusted_pubkey\": tp,\n195\t        \"folded_state\": folded.model_dump(),\n196\t    }\n197\t\n198\t\n199\t# ---- archive COPY (copy -> verify; NEVER a live drop here) ----------------------------------------------\n200\tdef archive_copy(store, K: int, snap_payload: dict, *, adir: Optional[Path] = None) -> dict:\n201\t    \"\"\"Copy the whole sealed segments below K into the archive (byte-identical), append their rows + this\n202\t    snapshot's row to `archive.manifest.json`, and VERIFY (per-segment sha256 + genesis-rooted re-attach:\n203\t    seg-0.first_prev_hash==GENESIS, each boundary_hash==next.first_prev_hash, last boundary_hash==\n204\t    base_prev_hash). Additive + idempotent (re-copying an already-archived segment is a no-op on identical\n205\t    bytes). Returns a report. Raises PruneUnsafe on any verification failure — leaving the live spine\n206\t    untouched (nothing is dropped here in any case).\"\"\"\n207\t    from .floor import floor_lock                            # a generic sibling-lockfile flock (reused)\n208\t    ad = adir if adir is not None else archive_dir()\n209\t    segdir = ad / \"segments\"\n210\t    segdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n211\t    archived = check_prune_safe(store, K)\n212\t    manifest_path = ad / \"archive.manifest.json\"\n213\t\n214\t    # Serialize the whole read-modify-write of archive.manifest.json + the segment copies, so two concurrent\n215\t    # archive_copy() calls cannot lose a manifest update or race on a segment file.\n216\t    with floor_lock(manifest_path):\n217\t        existing: dict[str, Any] = (json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n218\t                                    if manifest_path.exists()\n219\t                                    else {\"scope\": SCOPE, \"schema_version\": 1, \"segments\": [], \"snapshots\": []})\n220\t        have = {row[\"id\"] for row in existing[\"segments\"]}\n221\t        copied = _copy_segments(store, archived, existing, have, segdir, ad)\n222\t        existing[\"segments\"].sort(key=lambda r: r[\"first_seq\"])\n223\t        # append this snapshot's row (idempotent by cumulative root)\n224\t        srow = {k: snap_payload[k] for k in (\"base_seq\", \"base_count\", \"delta_merkle_root\",\n225\t                                             \"cumulative_merkle_root\", \"prev_snapshot_seq\")}\n226\t        if not any(s.get(\"cumulative_merkle_root\") == srow[\"cumulative_merkle_root\"]\n227\t                   for s in existing[\"snapshots\"]):\n228\t            existing[\"snapshots\"].append(srow)\n229\t        atomic_write_text(manifest_path, json.dumps(existing, indent=2), prefix=\".amanifest-\")\n230\t        fsync_dir(ad)\n231\t        _verify_archive_shape(existing, base_prev_hash=snap_payload[\"base_prev_hash\"])\n232\t    return {\"copied\": copied, \"archived_segments\": len(existing[\"segments\"]),\n233\t            \"archive_dir\": str(ad), \"verified\": True}\n234\t\n235\t\n236\tdef _copy_segments(store, archived: list[Segment], existing: dict, have: set,\n237\t                   segdir: Path, ad: Path) -> int:\n238\t    copied = 0\n239\t    for seg in archived:\n240\t        src = store._layout.seg_path(seg)\n241\t        dst = segdir / src.name\n242\t        sha = _sha256_file(src)\n243\t        row = {\"id\": seg.id, \"file\": f\"segments/{src.name}\", \"first_seq\": seg.first_seq,\n244\t               \"last_seq\": seg.last_seq, \"first_prev_hash\": seg.first_prev_hash,\n245\t               \"boundary_hash\": seg.boundary_hash, \"sha256\": sha,\n246\t               \"count\": (seg.last_seq or 0) - seg.first_seq + 1}\n247\t        if seg.id in have:\n248\t            prior_row = next(r for r in existing[\"segments\"] if r[\"id\"] == seg.id)\n249\t            # IDEMPOTENCY on CODEC-INVARIANT chain identity (first/last_seq + first_prev_hash + boundary_hash)\n250\t            # — stable across a live gzip-compaction that changes the segment's on-disk bytes (and thus its\n251\t            # sha256). Same identity -> keep the existing archived copy + its recorded sha256 untouched.\n252\t            same = all(prior_row.get(k) == row[k]\n253\t                       for k in (\"first_seq\", \"last_seq\", \"first_prev_hash\", \"boundary_hash\"))\n254\t            if not same:\n255\t                raise PruneUnsafe(f\"archive already holds seg {seg.id} with a DIFFERENT chain identity — \"\n256\t                                  f\"refusing to overwrite (possible corruption/rewrite)\")\n257\t            continue                                        # same segment already archived — idempotent no-op\n258\t        if not dst.exists():                                # copy -> fsync -> only then record (crash-safe)\n259\t            data = src.read_bytes()\n260\t            fd, tmp = _mkstemp(segdir, src.name)\n261\t            try:\n262\t                with os.fdopen(fd, \"wb\") as fo:\n263\t                    fo.write(data)\n264\t                    fo.flush()\n265\t                    os.fsync(fo.fileno())\n266\t                os.replace(tmp, dst)\n267\t            finally:\n268\t                Path(tmp).unlink(missing_ok=True)           # no-op if os.replace already consumed it\n269\t            fsync_dir(segdir)\n270\t        if _sha256_file(dst) != sha:\n271\t            raise PruneUnsafe(f\"archived copy of seg {seg.id} hashes differently than the source — aborting\")\n272\t        existing[\"segments\"].append(row)\n273\t        copied += 1\n274\t    return copied\n275\t\n276\t\n277\tdef _mkstemp(directory: Path, name: str):\n278\t    import tempfile\n279\t    return tempfile.mkstemp(dir=str(directory), prefix=f\".{name}.\", suffix=\".tmp\")\n280\t\n281\t\n282\tdef _verify_archive_shape(manifest: dict, *, base_prev_hash: str) -> None:\n283\t    \"\"\"The archived segment set must be a genesis-rooted, gap-free chain up to base_prev_hash.\"\"\"\n284\t    segs = sorted(manifest[\"segments\"], key=lambda r: r[\"first_seq\"])\n285\t    if not segs:\n286\t        raise PruneUnsafe(\"archive manifest has no segments\")\n287\t    if segs[0][\"first_prev_hash\"] != _GENESIS_PREV:\n288\t        raise PruneUnsafe(\"archive seg-0 does not link from genesis\")\n289\t    for a, b in zip(segs, segs[1:]):\n290\t        if a[\"boundary_hash\"] != b[\"first_prev_hash\"]:\n291\t            raise PruneUnsafe(f\"archive boundary break between seg {a['id']} and {b['id']}\")\n292\t        if a[\"last_seq\"] + 1 != b[\"first_seq\"]:\n293\t            raise PruneUnsafe(f\"archive seq gap between seg {a['id']} and {b['id']}\")\n294\t    if segs[-1][\"boundary_hash\"] != base_prev_hash:\n295\t        raise PruneUnsafe(\"archive tail boundary != the snapshot's base_prev_hash\")\n296\t\n297\t\n298\t# ---- re-attach verifier (`sigil spine verify --with-archive`) ------------------------------------------\n299\tdef verify_with_archive(store, *, adir: Optional[Path] = None) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n300\t    \"\"\"Prove the archived pruned prefix is AUTHENTIC — anchored to the OWNER SIGNATURE, never to the\n301\t    (attacker-controllable) archive manifest. Read-only; returns (ok, message). Checks, in order:\n302\t      (1) at-rest sha256 of every archived file;\n303\t      (2) the per-record CONTENT BINDING (verify_record: payload -> cert_digest AND entry_hash derivation)\n304\t          on every archived record — so a payload swap that preserves the chain fields is caught;\n305\t      (3) the LIVE spine head is a valid owner Ed25519 head (verify_checkpoint) — the anchor;\n306\t      (4) the archived prefix is a genesis-rooted contiguous chain (verify_chain);\n307\t      (5) the OWNER anchor, by whether a prune is committed in the signed head:\n308\t          • base_seq == 0 (no prune committed — the Slice-D capability state): the live spine STILL holds the\n309\t            archived prefix, so every archived record must EQUAL the owner-signed live record at that seq\n310\t            (entry_hash identity). The live head signs [0..T] incl. the prefix, so this ties the archive to\n311\t            the owner key.\n312\t          • base_seq  > 0 (a prune IS committed — Slice E): the live prefix is GONE. The archive must cover\n313\t            exactly [0..base_seq), its tail entry_hash must equal the owner-signed head.base_prev_hash, and\n314\t            the re-derived cumulative_merkle_root must equal the owner-signed head.cumulative_merkle_root;\n315\t            then [archive ‖ live] re-attaches into one genesis chain.\n316\t    Only after (5) is the manifest-vs-manifest Merkle re-derivation (6) a meaningful cross-check.\"\"\"\n317\t    from ..config import HEAD_PATH\n318\t    from ..reuse.models import SignedChainHead\n319\t    from .checkpoint import verify_checkpoint\n320\t    from .verify import verify_record\n321\t\n322\t    ad = adir if adir is not None else archive_dir()\n323\t    manifest_path = ad / \"archive.manifest.json\"\n324\t    if not manifest_path.exists():\n325\t        return False, f\"no archive manifest at {manifest_path}\"\n326\t    am = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n327\t    segrows = sorted(am[\"segments\"], key=lambda r: r[\"first_seq\"])\n328\t\n329\t    # (1) at-rest integrity + (2) per-record content BINDING (not just chain linkage).\n330\t    arch: list[SpineRecord] = []\n331\t    for row in segrows:\n332\t        p = ad / row[\"file\"]\n333\t        if not p.exists():\n334\t            return False, f\"archived segment {row['file']} is missing (pruned prefix unrecoverable)\"\n335\t        if _sha256_file(p) != row[\"sha256\"]:\n336\t            return False, f\"archived segment {row['file']} sha256 mismatch (at-rest tamper/corruption)\"\n337\t        for r in read_segment_records(p):\n338\t            bok, bwhy = verify_record(r)\n339\t            if not bok:\n340\t                return False, f\"archived record seq {r.seq} content binding failed: {bwhy}\"\n341\t            arch.append(r)\n342\t    arch.sort(key=lambda r: r.seq)\n343\t\n344\t    # (3) the live spine head must be owner-authentic — the anchor for everything below.\n345\t    hok, hmsg = verify_checkpoint(store)\n346\t    if not hok:\n347\t        return False, f\"live spine head does not verify ({hmsg}) — cannot anchor the archive to the owner key\"\n348\t    head = SignedChainHead.model_validate_json(HEAD_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")) if HEAD_PATH.exists() else None\n349\t    base_seq = head.base_seq if head else 0\n350\t    base_prev_hash = head.base_prev_hash if head else _GENESIS_PREV\n351\t    committed_cumulative = head.cumulative_merkle_root if head else \"\"\n352\t\n353\t    # (4) the archived prefix is a genesis-rooted contiguous chain on its own.\n354\t    aentries = [ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n355\t                for r in arch]\n356\t    aok, amsg = verify_chain(aentries)\n357\t    if not aok:\n358\t        return False, f\"archived prefix chain does not verify: {amsg}\"\n359\t\n360\t    # (5) OWNER anchor.\n361\t    if base_seq == 0:\n362\t        for r in arch:                                      # no prune committed: cross-check vs the live prefix\n363\t            live = store.get(r.seq)\n364\t            if live is None or live.entry_hash != r.entry_hash:\n365\t                return False, (f\"archived record seq {r.seq} does not match the owner-signed live spine \"\n366\t                               f\"(the archive is not a verified prefix of the current spine)\")\n367\t        anchor = \"against the owner-signed live prefix (no prune committed)\"\n368\t    else:                                                   # a prune IS committed (Slice E): live prefix gone\n369\t        if not arch or arch[-1].seq != base_seq - 1 or arch[0].seq != 0 or len(arch) != base_seq:\n370\t            return False, f\"archive does not cover exactly [0..{base_seq}) as the signed head declares\"\n371\t        if arch[-1].entry_hash != base_prev_hash:\n372\t            return False, \"archive tail entry_hash != the owner-signed head.base_prev_hash\"\n373\t        live_tail = [r for r in store.iter_records() if r.seq >= base_seq]\n374\t        chain = [ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n375\t                 for r in (arch + live_tail)]\n376\t        cok, cmsg = verify_chain(chain)\n377\t        if not cok:\n378\t            return False, f\"re-attached [archive‖live] chain does not verify: {cmsg}\"\n379\t        rederived = _cumulative_over(arch, am)\n380\t        if rederived != committed_cumulative:\n381\t            return False, (\"re-derived cumulative_merkle_root != the owner-signed head.cumulative_merkle_root \"\n382\t                           \"(archived leaves are not what the owner committed)\")\n383\t        anchor = \"against owner-signed head.cumulative_merkle_root + base_prev_hash\"\n384\t\n385\t    # (6) manifest self-consistency: each committed delta/cumulative re-derives from the real archived leaves.\n386\t    by_seq = {r.seq: r for r in arch}\n387\t    prior_cumulative, k_prev = \"\", 0\n388\t    for srow in sorted(am[\"snapshots\"], key=lambda s: s[\"base_seq\"]):\n389\t        K = srow[\"base_seq\"]\n390\t        if any(s not in by_seq for s in range(k_prev, K)):\n391\t            return False, f\"archive is missing leaves for snapshot base_seq {K}\"\n392\t        delta = merkle_root([by_seq[s].entry_hash for s in range(k_prev, K)])\n393\t        cumulative = chain_cumulative(prior_cumulative, delta)\n394\t        if delta != srow[\"delta_merkle_root\"] or cumulative != srow[\"cumulative_merkle_root\"]:\n395\t            return False, f\"Merkle root mismatch at snapshot base_seq {K} (archive manifest inconsistent)\"\n396\t        prior_cumulative, k_prev = cumulative, K\n397\t    return True, (f\"archive verified {anchor}: {len(segrows)} segment(s), {len(arch)} records bound + \"\n398\t                  f\"chained from genesis; {len(am['snapshots'])} Merkle root(s) re-derived\")\n399\t\n400\t\n401\tdef _cumulative_over(arch: list[SpineRecord], am: dict) -> str:\n402\t    \"\"\"The running cumulative over the full archived prefix, re-derived per committed snapshot boundary.\"\"\"\n403\t    by_seq = {r.seq: r for r in arch}\n404\t    cumulative, k_prev = \"\", 0\n405\t    for srow in sorted(am[\"snapshots\"], key=lambda s: s[\"base_seq\"]):\n406\t        K = srow[\"base_seq\"]\n407\t        cumulative = chain_cumulative(cumulative, merkle_root([by_seq[s].entry_hash for s in range(k_prev, K)]))\n408\t        k_prev = K\n409\t    return cumulative\n410\t\n411\t\n412\t# entry_hash recomputation guard used by tests (a leaf whose entry_hash != H(seq,prev,cert) is tampered).\n413\tdef recompute_entry_hash(r: SpineRecord) -> str:\n414\t    return _entry_hash(r.seq, r.prev_hash, r.cert_digest)\n415\t"}]},"uuid":"5b2ffb74-f487-4514-9166-8d52fd350cbd","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:00.281Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"bd278812-eb15-42e6-a693-fb78b9b1fa7a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"12c1acd1-308c-4787-b1e4-e8f7006300c5","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015VXA47r2cUcNYnoqUFznqK","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Daily signed checkpoint of the spine head (SIGIL §6.3.6, WARDEN anchor).\n2\t\n3\tGenerates + persists a solo-owner Ed25519 keypair once (1-of-1 trust root), then signs\n4\tthe chain head so the whole spine is anchored: rewriting history would require forging\n5\tthe owner signature, and a shrunk/back-dated head is caught by the monotonic last_seq.\n6\t\n7\tNote: for a personal, single-owner, local-first system the owner's signing key lives\n8\tlocally (0600) — a deliberate simplification of the framework's \"keys never on the\n9\truntime host\" rule, appropriate to this threat model (§1.3). A hardware co-signer can be\n10\tadded later by raising the trust-root threshold.\n11\t\"\"\"\n12\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n13\t\n14\timport json\n15\timport os\n16\tfrom pathlib import Path\n17\t\n18\t_MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA = 2   # head schema versions this build understands; a higher one -> \"upgrade sigil\"\n19\t\n20\tfrom ..config import HEAD_PATH, KEYS_DIR, OWNER_KEY_ID, SCOPE\n21\tfrom ..reuse import (\n22\t    AuthorizerKey,\n23\t    SignedChainHead,\n24\t    TrustRoot,\n25\t    generate_keypair,\n26\t    sign_head,\n27\t    verify_head,\n28\t)\n29\tfrom .atomicio import atomic_write_text\n30\tfrom .floor import Floor, FloorDowngrade, advance_floor, check_floor, floor_lock, load_floor\n31\tfrom .store import SpineStore\n32\t\n33\t_PRIV = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.priv\"\n34\t_PUB = KEYS_DIR / \"owner.pub\"\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tdef _atomic_write_text(path: Path, data: str) -> None:\n38\t    \"\"\"Durably + atomically replace `path` with `data` (FIX 3) — a reader sees either the whole old or\n39\t    the whole new head, never a torn one, and a crash leaves the previous valid head intact. Delegates to\n40\t    the one shared `spine.atomicio.atomic_write_text` so the head, the manifest, and every cutover use the\n41\t    same audited routine (no drift).\"\"\"\n42\t    atomic_write_text(path, data, prefix=\".head-\")\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef _owner_keys() -> tuple[str, str]:\n46\t    if not _PRIV.exists():\n47\t        KEYS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n48\t        kp = generate_keypair()\n49\t        _PRIV.write_text(kp.private_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n50\t        os.chmod(_PRIV, 0o600)\n51\t        _PUB.write_text(kp.public_key_b64, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n52\t    return _PRIV.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip(), _PUB.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").strip()\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef trust_root() -> TrustRoot:\n56\t    _, pub = _owner_keys()\n57\t    return TrustRoot(threshold=1, authorizers=[\n58\t        AuthorizerKey(key_id=OWNER_KEY_ID, name=\"owner\", public_key_b64=pub)])\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef _read_head_on_disk() -> SignedChainHead | None:\n62\t    \"\"\"The current head.json parsed, or None (absent / unparseable). Used only as a best-effort monotonic\n63\t    race guard — never a security boundary (the floor is).\"\"\"\n64\t    try:\n65\t        if not HEAD_PATH.exists():\n66\t            return None\n67\t        return SignedChainHead.model_validate_json(HEAD_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n68\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — a corrupt/future-schema on-disk head has no comparable count\n69\t        return None\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tdef checkpoint(store: SpineStore | None = None, *, force: bool = False) -> SignedChainHead:\n73\t    \"\"\"Sign the spine head + advance the durable anti-rollback floor as one critical section under a single\n74\t    `floor_lock` (best-effort on non-POSIX / an unwritable home — see floor_lock), so two racing\n75\t    checkpoint() callers do not leave head.json and floor.json inconsistent, and neither the head nor the\n76\t    floor is rolled BACKWARDS by a stale concurrent signer. `force=True` (the `sigil floor reset` path)\n77\t    re-anchors a deliberately SHORTER spine — it skips the monotonic head guard; the caller then\n78\t    force-lowers the floor.\"\"\"\n79\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t    priv, _ = _owner_keys()\n81\t    head = sign_head(store.entries(), engagement_slug=SCOPE, signers=[(OWNER_KEY_ID, priv)])\n82\t    with floor_lock():                                      # serialize head+floor commit across all callers\n83\t        if not force:\n84\t            # MONOTONIC HEAD GUARD: never roll head.json back to a shorter head — a concurrent checkpoint()\n85\t            # that already committed a LONGER head (and advanced the floor to it) would otherwise be undone\n86\t            # on disk, and the (higher) floor would then spurious-TAMPER the shorter head we wrote.\n87\t            disk = _read_head_on_disk()\n88\t            if disk is not None and disk.entry_count > head.entry_count:\n89\t                return disk                                 # our sign is stale; the newer head stands\n90\t        _atomic_write_text(HEAD_PATH, head.model_dump_json())   # FIX 3: never leave a torn head on a crash\n91\t        # Advance the floor UNDER THE SAME LOCK (re-loads prior inside advance -> last-writer-monotonic).\n92\t        # BEST-EFFORT so a floor problem never bricks signing (consolidate + warden-anchor route here too),\n93\t        # AND a not-advanced floor only ever REJECTS MORE — never a false-CLEAN — so warning is fail-SAFE.\n94\t        try:\n95\t            advance_floor(head, _locked=True)\n96\t        except FloorDowngrade as e:                         # the INTENDED downward-refusal (e.g. post-reset) — benign\n97\t            import sys\n98\t            print(f\"warning: durable anti-rollback floor not advanced ({e}); run `sigil floor reset` after \"\n99\t                  f\"a deliberate reset/restore\", file=sys.stderr)\n100\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a REAL IO/write/corrupt-or-wrong-scope-load failure — surface loudly\n101\t            import sys\n102\t            print(f\"ERROR: durable anti-rollback floor WRITE FAILED ({e}) — the floor may be stale; \"\n103\t                  f\"investigate before trusting `sigil verify`\", file=sys.stderr)\n104\t    return head\n105\t\n106\t\n107\tdef classify_head(head: SignedChainHead, entries: list, tr: TrustRoot,\n108\t                  *, floor: Floor | None = None) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n109\t    \"\"\"Pure head/chain classification (no globals — testable in isolation), SNAPSHOT-AWARE for the\n110\t    cold-archive hard-prune. The live window is selected BY SEQ (`seq >= base_seq`), not by array prefix, so\n111\t    every crash-cutover state verifies clean. Failures are TAMPERING (front-truncation, tail-truncation, or\n112\t    a rewrite); a chain grown past the anchor is benign-stale. entry_count stays ABSOLUTE (base_count +\n113\t    live). For a v1 head (base_seq=0, base_count=0) this is BYTE-IDENTICAL to the pre-v2 semantics.\"\"\"\n114\t    base = head.base_count\n115\t    signed_live = head.entry_count - base\n116\t    live = [e for e in entries if e.seq >= head.base_seq]\n117\t    if len(live) < signed_live:\n118\t        return False, (f\"TAMPERING: live window has {len(live)} records but the signed head anchors \"\n119\t                       f\"{signed_live} (truncated/rolled back)\")\n120\t    if signed_live > 0:\n121\t        # LEFT-EDGE PIN — UNCONDITIONAL TAMPERING: the signed live window must start EXACTLY at base_seq and\n122\t        # link from base_prev_hash. This closes the false-CLEAN front-drop hole (the n<entry_count no-op\n123\t        # trap transposed to the left edge) — an attacker cannot drop the front of the live window. (Only\n124\t        # the window-SHAPE check is gated on signed_live>0; the SIGNATURE check below is not.)\n125\t        if live[0].seq != head.base_seq or live[0].prev_hash != head.base_prev_hash:\n126\t            return False, \"TAMPERING: live window does not start at the signed base (front-truncated/rolled back)\"\n127\t    # SIGNATURE / threshold check runs UNCONDITIONALLY — including signed_live == 0 (an empty live window, the\n128\t    # case a forged zero-anchor head presents). verify_head over an empty window still validates\n129\t    # head_hash==base_prev_hash, last_seq==base_seq, entry_count==base_count AND the owner Ed25519 signature,\n130\t    # so a forged UNSIGNED zero head is TAMPERING (the whole tamper-evidence point), while a legitimately\n131\t    # owner-signed empty spine still passes. (An earlier version gated this behind signed_live>0 and thereby\n132\t    # skipped signature verification for empty heads — a keyless tamper-alert-suppression fail-open.)\n133\t    ok, msg = verify_head(head, live[:signed_live], tr, genesis_prev=head.base_prev_hash)\n134\t    if not ok:\n135\t        return False, f\"TAMPERING: history rewritten at or below the signed head — {msg}\"\n136\t    # DURABLE EXTERNAL FLOOR (hard-prune C1) — runs AFTER the in-band signature check, so it only ever\n137\t    # ADDS a rejection: a VALIDLY-signed but STALE head is caught here by the monotonic floor / meta-chain,\n138\t    # which the in-band signature cannot catch on its own. HONEST SCOPE (do not overclaim): this catches a\n139\t    # stale head ONLY when `floor` is a NEWER value the attacker did NOT roll back — i.e. an OUT-OF-BAND\n140\t    # verifier holding a retained floor (a paired device over WireGuard), or the routine `--reset` path\n141\t    # (the floor survives the spine-dir rmtree). It does NOT stop a same-host attacker who can also rewrite\n142\t    # the UNSIGNED floor.json: the local verify reads the floor fresh from that same attacker-controlled\n143\t    # disk, rolling head.json and floor.json back together (see floor.py HONEST LIMIT). No floor -> pass\n144\t    # (byte-identical to pre-floor).\n145\t    ok_floor, floor_msg = check_floor(head, floor)\n146\t    if not ok_floor:\n147\t        return False, f\"TAMPERING: {floor_msg}\"\n148\t    if len(live) > signed_live:\n149\t        return True, f\"anchors {signed_live} records; {len(live) - signed_live} appended since — run `sigil sign` to re-anchor\"\n150\t    return True, f\"anchors all {signed_live} records (current)\"\n151\t\n152\t\n153\tdef verify_checkpoint(store: SpineStore | None = None) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n154\t    store = store or SpineStore()\n155\t    if not HEAD_PATH.exists():\n156\t        return False, \"no signed head — run `sigil sign` to anchor the spine\"\n157\t    raw = HEAD_PATH.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n158\t    try:\n159\t        head = SignedChainHead.model_validate_json(raw)     # a future-schema head fails extra=\"forbid\" here\n160\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — never crash + never false-clean: distinguish \"too new\" from \"corrupt\"\n161\t        try:\n162\t            sv = int(json.loads(raw).get(\"schema_version\", 1))\n163\t        except (ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):\n164\t            return False, \"corrupt head — cannot parse (run `sigil sign` to re-anchor)\"\n165\t        if sv > _MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA:\n166\t            return False, f\"head schema v{sv} too new — upgrade sigil (never treated as clean)\"\n167\t        return False, \"corrupt head — cannot parse (run `sigil sign` to re-anchor)\"\n168\t    if head.schema_version > _MAX_HEAD_SCHEMA:\n169\t        return False, f\"head schema v{head.schema_version} too new — upgrade sigil (never treated as clean)\"\n170\t    try:\n171\t        floor = load_floor()                                # None if absent -> byte-identical to pre-floor\n172\t    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a PRESENT-but-corrupt floor is suspicious; fail CLOSED, never clean\n173\t        return False, f\"durable anti-rollback floor unreadable — refuse to certify (possible tamper): {e}\"\n174\t    return classify_head(head, store.entries(), trust_root(), floor=floor)\n175\t"}]},"uuid":"82914fb3-55e5-4d2f-a01e-71bf27f6ec0c","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:05.812Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"12c1acd1-308c-4787-b1e4-e8f7006300c5","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"ba016376-cbb1-41be-984f-fbadac92f1b3","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01StyLFkZFkeHASd5jXmw6RJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"verify_record (Phase 7, P0.2) — the single \"prove-this-atom\" helper reused by the live tail,\n2\tthe UI `/api/record` endpoint, and the mobile daemon, so \"serve-the-quote / prove-don't-guess\" is\n3\tONE implementation, not three. It performs the two per-atom checks the store's `verify()` does for\n4\tthe whole log, on a single record:\n5\t\n6\t  1. BINDING — the record's content still hashes to its stored `cert_digest` (catches a silent\n7\t     payload edit).\n8\t  2. DERIVATION — `entry_hash` is correctly derived from (seq, prev_hash, cert_digest) (catches an\n9\t     entry-field tamper).\n10\t\n11\tCross-record LINKAGE (prev_hash == the predecessor's entry_hash) needs the predecessor and is done\n12\tby `SpineTailer` as records stream. Reuses `reuse.digest_payload` and the canonical `_entry_hash`\n13\tfrom `reuse.chain` verbatim — never a re-implementation that could drift from the enforced path.\n14\t\n15\tSCOPE (honest): these are UNKEYED sha-256 checks. They catch accidental corruption and a naive\n16\tstale-field tamper, but they do NOT prove AUTHENTICITY against a writer who can recompute the\n17\tdigests (a tip tamper or a forward-cascaded fork stays internally consistent). Resistance to that\n18\trequires the owner-SIGNED head — `SpineTailer.check_anchor` (`checkpoint.classify_head`/`verify_head`),\n19\twhich is what marks a record `anchored`. Treat a passing `verify_record` as well-formedness, not proof.\"\"\"\n20\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n21\t\n22\tfrom ..reuse import digest_payload\n23\tfrom ..reuse.chain import _entry_hash\n24\t\n25\t\n26\tdef verify_record(record) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n27\t    \"\"\"(ok, reason) for a single SpineRecord — binding + entry-hash derivation. Fail-closed.\"\"\"\n28\t    content = {\n29\t        \"scope\": record.scope, \"kind\": record.kind, \"source\": record.source, \"actor\": record.actor,\n30\t        \"payload\": record.payload, \"parent_id\": record.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": record.supersedes_id,\n31\t    }\n32\t    if digest_payload(content) != record.cert_digest:\n33\t        return False, f\"binding break at seq {record.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n34\t    if _entry_hash(record.seq, record.prev_hash, record.cert_digest) != record.entry_hash:\n35\t        return False, f\"entry_hash mismatch at seq {record.seq}: derivation broken (entry tampered)\"\n36\t    return True, f\"seq {record.seq} binds and derives cleanly\"\n37\t"}]},"uuid":"dc205ac1-5696-4939-9613-f1ed39f5190c","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:06.493Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ba016376-cbb1-41be-984f-fbadac92f1b3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"61948127-2a36-40d2-b040-cb75766bfee0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BduAg5wSMk1jFiKqeqHfwM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Tamper-evident, hash-linked spine chain.\n2\t\n3\tVENDORED VERBATIM from CRUCIBLE `framework/v2/evidence/chain.py` (owner's own work).\n4\tEach `ChainEntry` hashes (seq, prev_hash, cert_digest); a `SignedChainHead` anchors the\n5\twhole log with a monotonic `last_seq` anti-rollback. Deterministic + append-only; signing\n6\tthe head is provisioning, the runtime verifies. Only the imports are localised.\n7\t\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\tfrom .canonical import canonical_json, evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n11\tfrom .crypto import sign, verify_threshold\n12\tfrom .models import _GENESIS_PREV, ChainEntry, Signature, SignedChainHead, TrustRoot\n13\t\n14\t\n15\tdef _entry_hash(seq: int, prev_hash: str, cert_digest: str) -> str:\n16\t    return sha256_hex(canonical_json({\"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": prev_hash, \"seq\": seq}))\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef build_chain(cert_digests: list[str], *, start_seq: int = 0) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n20\t    entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n21\t    prev = _GENESIS_PREV\n22\t    for i, cd in enumerate(cert_digests):\n23\t        seq = start_seq + i\n24\t        eh = _entry_hash(seq, prev, cd)\n25\t        entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=seq, prev_hash=prev, cert_digest=cd, entry_hash=eh))\n26\t        prev = eh\n27\t    return entries\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef append_entry(entries: list[ChainEntry], cert_digest: str) -> ChainEntry:\n31\t    prev = entries[-1].entry_hash if entries else _GENESIS_PREV\n32\t    seq = (entries[-1].seq + 1) if entries else 0\n33\t    return ChainEntry(seq=seq, prev_hash=prev, cert_digest=cert_digest,\n34\t                      entry_hash=_entry_hash(seq, prev, cert_digest))\n35\t\n36\t\n37\t_HEAD_V2_FIELDS = (\"base_seq\", \"base_prev_hash\", \"base_count\", \"cumulative_merkle_root\",\n38\t                   \"snapshot_seq\", \"prev_head_hash\")\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef verify_chain(entries: list[ChainEntry], *, genesis_prev: str = _GENESIS_PREV) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n42\t    prev = genesis_prev                              # _GENESIS_PREV for a full chain; base_prev_hash for a\n43\t    #                                                  re-based (pruned) live window — links cleanly, no fork\n44\t    for i, e in enumerate(entries):\n45\t        if e.prev_hash != prev:\n46\t            return False, f\"chain break at seq {e.seq}: prev_hash mismatch (entry deleted/reordered)\"\n47\t        if e.entry_hash != _entry_hash(e.seq, e.prev_hash, e.cert_digest):\n48\t            return False, f\"chain break at seq {e.seq}: entry_hash mismatch (entry tampered)\"\n49\t        if i > 0 and e.seq != entries[i - 1].seq + 1:\n50\t            return False, f\"chain break: seq gap at {e.seq}\"\n51\t        prev = e.entry_hash\n52\t    return True, f\"{len(entries)} entr{'y' if len(entries) == 1 else 'ies'} link cleanly\"\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef _head_payload(head: SignedChainHead) -> dict:\n56\t    d = head.model_dump(mode=\"json\")\n57\t    d.pop(\"signatures\", None)\n58\t    # Version-conditional signing payload: a v1 head (schema_version < 2) drops the v2 fields ENTIRELY, so\n59\t    # its signing bytes are BYTE-IDENTICAL to a pre-v2 head — every persisted head.json and every old client\n60\t    # verifies unchanged. Explicit pop, NOT model_dump(exclude_unset): model_validate_json marks defaulted\n61\t    # fields \"set\", so exclude_unset would re-emit them and false-alarm every existing deployment.\n62\t    if int(d.get(\"schema_version\", 1)) < 2:\n63\t        for f in _HEAD_V2_FIELDS:\n64\t            d.pop(f, None)\n65\t    return d\n66\t\n67\t\n68\tdef sign_head(entries: list[ChainEntry], *, engagement_slug: str, signers: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> SignedChainHead:\n69\t    head_hash = entries[-1].entry_hash if entries else _GENESIS_PREV\n70\t    last_seq = entries[-1].seq if entries else 0\n71\t    head = SignedChainHead(engagement_slug=engagement_slug, last_seq=last_seq,\n72\t                           entry_count=len(entries), head_hash=head_hash)\n73\t    msg = evidence_signing_bytes(_head_payload(head))\n74\t    sigs = [Signature(key_id=kid, signature_b64=sign(priv, msg)) for kid, priv in signers]\n75\t    return head.model_copy(update={\"signatures\": sigs})\n76\t\n77\t\n78\tdef verify_head(head: SignedChainHead, entries: list[ChainEntry], trust_root: TrustRoot,\n79\t                *, prev_highwater: int | None = None, genesis_prev: str = _GENESIS_PREV) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n80\t    ok_chain, reason = verify_chain(entries, genesis_prev=genesis_prev)\n81\t    if not ok_chain:\n82\t        return False, reason\n83\t    exp_hash = entries[-1].entry_hash if entries else genesis_prev\n84\t    exp_seq = entries[-1].seq if entries else head.base_seq\n85\t    # entry_count stays ABSOLUTE: it must equal base_count (pruned records the head commits by Merkle root)\n86\t    # plus the length of the live window it was signed over. base_count == 0 for a v1 head, so this reduces\n87\t    # to the pre-v2 `entry_count == len(entries)` exactly.\n88\t    if head.head_hash != exp_hash or head.last_seq != exp_seq or head.entry_count != head.base_count + len(entries):\n89\t        return False, \"head does not match the chain (log truncated or head rewritten)\"\n90\t    thr = verify_threshold(evidence_signing_bytes(_head_payload(head)), head.signatures, trust_root)\n91\t    if not thr.satisfied:\n92\t        return False, f\"head signature invalid: {thr.reason}\"\n93\t    if prev_highwater is not None and head.last_seq < prev_highwater:\n94\t        return False, f\"rollback rejected: head last_seq {head.last_seq} < accepted high-water {prev_highwater}\"\n95\t    return True, f\"chain of {len(entries)} entr{'y' if len(entries)==1 else 'ies'} anchored by a valid signed head\"\n96\t"}]},"uuid":"750b50c3-8e40-4706-ada7-81035b0724a4","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:06.709Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"61948127-2a36-40d2-b040-cb75766bfee0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"eefafdcd-a838-45ec-bdad-f612b4e925f7","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdCNtoBU8s9ERiV3qPZy5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0162GBsitpxJXdh9Bvmb6xgh","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/merkle.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8420,"cache_read_input_tokens":33049,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8420,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdCNtmsLd8kn5RbWmM9dw","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"415b23e5-21b9-4a9f-82d5-9c348774b549","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:13.156Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"415b23e5-21b9-4a9f-82d5-9c348774b549","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0162GBsitpxJXdh9Bvmb6xgh","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Merkle accumulator for the cold-archive hard-prune (C2).\n2\t\n3\tEach prune commits, in its owner-signed `kind=\"snapshot\"` record, a `delta_merkle_root` (a binary Merkle\n4\ttree over THIS prune's new leaves = the `entry_hash` of every pruned record `[K_prev..K-1]`, in seq order)\n5\tand a running `cumulative_merkle_root = H(prior_cumulative ‖ delta)`. The accumulator lets prune #N commit\n6\twhat the archive holds WITHOUT mounting the offline archive, and lets any verifier handed the archive\n7\tre-derive each `delta` from the real archived leaves and re-chain the accumulator to prove the pruned\n8\tprefix is exactly what was committed — any single pruned record carries a Merkle inclusion proof.\n9\t\n10\tDesign choices that matter for soundness:\n11\t- DOMAIN SEPARATION: a leaf is hashed with a `L:` prefix, an interior node with `N:`, so no interior hash\n12\t  can be presented as a leaf (a second-preimage class this closes).\n13\t- DUP-LAST-ON-ODD (C2): an odd level duplicates its LAST node before pairing. This is the classic CVE-2012-2459\n14\t  ambiguity source ONLY when the tree size is not otherwise bound — here the leaf COUNT (`base_count`) is\n15\t  committed in the signed head alongside the root, so a re-derivation over a different leaf multiset that\n16\t  happens to collide the root is still rejected by the count mismatch. We keep dup-last for simplicity and\n17\t  bind the count out-of-band.\n18\t- Deterministic + offline: no wallclock, no randomness — a verifier re-runs it byte-identically.\n19\t\"\"\"\n20\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n21\t\n22\tfrom ..reuse.canonical import sha256_hex\n23\t\n24\t_LEAF = \"L:\"   # domain tag for a leaf\n25\t_NODE = \"N:\"   # domain tag for an interior node\n26\t_ACC = \"C:\"    # domain tag for the cumulative accumulator step\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tdef merkle_root(leaves: list[str]) -> str:\n30\t    \"\"\"Binary Merkle root over hex-string `leaves` (the pruned records' `entry_hash`, in seq order). Odd\n31\t    levels duplicate the LAST node. Empty leaves -> \"\" (the no-prune identity). Pure + deterministic.\"\"\"\n32\t    if not leaves:\n33\t        return \"\"\n34\t    level = [sha256_hex((_LEAF + leaf).encode(\"utf-8\")) for leaf in leaves]\n35\t    while len(level) > 1:\n36\t        if len(level) % 2 == 1:\n37\t            level.append(level[-1])                         # dup-last-on-odd\n38\t        level = [sha256_hex((_NODE + level[i] + level[i + 1]).encode(\"utf-8\"))\n39\t                 for i in range(0, len(level), 2)]\n40\t    return level[0]\n41\t\n42\t\n43\tdef chain_cumulative(prior_cumulative: str, delta_root: str) -> str:\n44\t    \"\"\"The running accumulator step: `H(prior ‖ delta)`. `prior_cumulative == \"\"` for the first prune, so\n45\t    the first cumulative is `H(\"\" ‖ delta0)` — distinct from `delta0` itself (domain-tagged), so a verifier\n46\t    cannot confuse the first accumulator with a bare delta.\"\"\"\n47\t    return sha256_hex((_ACC + prior_cumulative + \"|\" + delta_root).encode(\"utf-8\"))\n48\t"}]},"uuid":"e071711e-e1fb-4e6f-a9c0-ebc426516ab6","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:13.202Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"415b23e5-21b9-4a9f-82d5-9c348774b549","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
