{"parentUuid":"17e62d93-30db-4091-8a25-67806f830fcc","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdCNtoBU8s9ERiV3qPZy5","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DxgGrmcf9WjPZsGf2uyQR6","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/manifest.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8420,"cache_read_input_tokens":33049,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8420,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdCNtmsLd8kn5RbWmM9dw","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"e1d87c59-0918-41f9-bd34-3b4f15af9c9e","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:13.584Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"17e62d93-30db-4091-8a25-67806f830fcc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SHjpTEJpJQ6funM3gTrx7i","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Durable external anti-rollback floor (cold-archive hard-prune, C1).\n2\t\n3\t`~/.sigil/floor.json`, mode 0600, at `SIGIL_HOME` root **OUTSIDE `spine/`** so `SpineStore.reset()` /\n4\t`sigil ingest --reset` (which rmtree the spine dir) can never lower it — a routine reset shortening the\n5\tspine cannot roll the durable floor back under a warm / returning-paired verifier. A sibling\n6\t`floor.json.lock` (empty, 0600) serializes concurrent advances; back-up / audit tooling should expect it.\n7\t\n8\tWHY a second, out-of-band witness: `head.json` is a plain file an attacker can overwrite with a\n9\tGENUINELY-OLD owner-signed head — a stale rollback the in-band Ed25519 signature alone cannot catch,\n10\tbecause that old head was validly signed. The floor's monotonic {last_seq, base_seq, base_count} plus\n11\tthe `prev_head_hash` meta-chain catch that replay for any verifier that has ever seen a newer floor.\n12\tThe floor only ever ADDS rejections (it is checked AFTER the in-band signature check), so with no floor\n13\tpresent, or values it satisfies, behavior is byte-identical to the pre-floor spine.\n14\t\n15\tHONEST LIMIT (accepted, §1.3) — do NOT overclaim: the owner key is FS-resident AND `floor.json` is an\n16\tUNSIGNED file at 0600, so a SAME-HOST attacker with the owner's UID (or root) — even WITHOUT the Ed25519\n17\tkey — defeats the LOCAL verify path by rewriting head.json AND floor.json together (the local verify reads\n18\tthe floor fresh from that same attacker-controlled disk). The floor's real anti-rollback guarantee holds\n19\tonly for (i) the routine `--reset` path (the floor lives OUTSIDE spine/, so a spine-dir rmtree can't lower\n20\tit), (ii) an OUT-OF-BAND verifier that retained a newer floor (a paired device over WireGuard), and\n21\t(iii) an attacker who can overwrite only head.json, not the floor. A COLD verifier bootstrapping off an\n22\tuntrusted mirror that never authenticates the floor even once is unprotected — class-identical to today's\n23\tsingle-file rollback, NOT widened by pruning. Bounded (not eliminated) by seeding the initial floor over\n24\tthe authenticated WireGuard pairing channel, never the mirror.\n25\t\"\"\"\n26\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n27\t\n28\timport os\n29\tfrom contextlib import contextmanager\n30\tfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\n31\tfrom pathlib import Path\n32\tfrom typing import Iterator, Optional\n33\t\n34\tfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field\n35\t\n36\tfrom ..config import FLOOR_PATH, SCOPE\n37\tfrom ..reuse import SignedChainHead\n38\tfrom ..reuse.canonical import evidence_signing_bytes, sha256_hex\n39\tfrom ..reuse.chain import _head_payload\n40\tfrom .atomicio import atomic_write_text\n41\t\n42\ttry:\n43\t    import fcntl\n44\texcept ImportError:  # pragma: no cover — non-POSIX; floor advance falls back to best-effort (single host)\n45\t    fcntl = None  # type: ignore[assignment]\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tclass FloorDowngrade(ValueError):\n49\t    \"\"\"The INTENDED refusal: an advance whose head would lower the floor / break the meta-chain (e.g. a\n50\t    stale concurrent signer, or re-signing a shorter spine after a reset). Distinct from a load/write\n51\t    FAILURE (a corrupt or wrong-scope floor, an IO error) so `checkpoint()` can treat this one as benign\n52\t    (warn) while surfacing a genuine failure loudly.\"\"\"\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tclass Floor(BaseModel):\n56\t    \"\"\"The persisted anti-rollback watermark. Non-secret (hashes + seqs), but written 0600 as\n57\t    defense-in-depth. `head_sig_hash` is the meta-chain identity of the CURRENT accepted head. The\n58\t    monotonic quantity is `entry_count` (ABSOLUTE = base_count + live, unchanged across a prune) — it,\n59\t    not `last_seq`, is the sound rollback guard: `last_seq` is 0-indexed and is 0 for BOTH an empty spine\n60\t    and a 1-record spine, so a 1->0 rollback would slip past a last_seq-only check.\"\"\"\n61\t    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"forbid\")\n62\t    schema_version: int = 1\n63\t    scope: str\n64\t    entry_count: int = Field(ge=0)   # ABSOLUTE record count — the primary monotonic anti-rollback quantity\n65\t    last_seq: int = Field(ge=0)\n66\t    base_seq: int = Field(ge=0)\n67\t    base_count: int = Field(ge=0)\n68\t    head_sig_hash: str\n69\t    updated_ts: str = \"\"\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tdef head_sig_hash(head: SignedChainHead) -> str:\n73\t    \"\"\"The identity of an ACCEPTED head in the meta-chain: sha256 of its owner-signing bytes (the SAME\n74\t    bytes `verify_threshold` checks) — a hash-space distinct from `head_hash` (which hashes the chain\n75\t    tip). A v1 head hashes its v1 payload (v2 fields dropped by `_head_payload`), so the hash a v2\n76\t    first-prune head names as `prev_head_hash` matches the recorded floor exactly across the schema bump.\n77\t    \"\"\"\n78\t    return sha256_hex(evidence_signing_bytes(_head_payload(head)))\n79\t\n80\t\n81\tdef load_floor(path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Optional[Floor]:\n82\t    \"\"\"Read the durable floor. `None` if ABSENT (pre-floor / never-signed spine — floor checks are then\n83\t    skipped, byte-identical to before). RAISES on a PRESENT-but-unreadable floor: a corrupt or\n84\t    forbid-violating floor is suspicious (tamper / partial write survived), and must never be silently\n85\t    treated as absent — that would be a fail-open un-flooring. Callers on the verify path catch the raise\n86\t    and fail CLOSED with a distinct message; they never treat it as clean.\"\"\"\n87\t    p = Path(path) if path is not None else FLOOR_PATH\n88\t    if not p.exists():\n89\t        return None\n90\t    fl = Floor.model_validate_json(p.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n91\t    if fl.scope != SCOPE:\n92\t        # A floor for a DIFFERENT scope must not silently govern this spine (a swapped-in / cross-store\n93\t        # floor). Suspicious -> raise, so the read path fails CLOSED exactly like a corrupt floor.\n94\t        raise ValueError(f\"durable floor scope {fl.scope!r} != active scope {SCOPE!r} (wrong-store floor)\")\n95\t    return fl\n96\t\n97\t\n98\tdef check_floor(head: SignedChainHead, floor: Optional[Floor]) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n99\t    \"\"\"Reject rules. A validly-signed head that nonetheless violates the durable floor is a ROLLBACK\n100\t    (stale-head replay / un-prune / older-snapshot replay), reported as TAMPERING by the caller. No\n101\t    floor -> pass (byte-identical). Pure + side-effect-free, so it is testable in isolation and shared\n102\t    by the write path (`advance_floor`'s downgrade guard, RE-loaded under the lock) and every read path.\"\"\"\n103\t    if floor is None:\n104\t        return True, \"no durable floor\"\n105\t    if head.entry_count < floor.entry_count:            # PRIMARY guard: absolute count (prune-invariant)\n106\t        return False, (f\"ROLLBACK: head entry_count {head.entry_count} < durable floor entry_count \"\n107\t                       f\"{floor.entry_count} (truncated spine / stale head replay)\")\n108\t    if head.last_seq < floor.last_seq:\n109\t        return False, (f\"ROLLBACK: head last_seq {head.last_seq} < durable floor last_seq \"\n110\t                       f\"{floor.last_seq} (stale head / truncated spine replay)\")\n111\t    if head.base_seq < floor.base_seq:\n112\t        return False, (f\"UN-PRUNE: head base_seq {head.base_seq} < durable floor base_seq \"\n113\t                       f\"{floor.base_seq} (older-snapshot replay)\")\n114\t    if head.base_count < floor.base_count:\n115\t        return False, (f\"UN-PRUNE: head base_count {head.base_count} < durable floor base_count \"\n116\t                       f\"{floor.base_count} (older-snapshot replay)\")\n117\t    if head.schema_version >= 2:\n118\t        # META-CHAIN (v2 heads only — a v1 head carries no prev_head_hash so this is dormant until the\n119\t        # first prune). This enforces CORRECT-PARENT LINKAGE: a v2 head is accepted iff it IS the recorded\n120\t        # accepted head (re-verify) OR links to it via prev_head_hash (its child). It blocks replay of a\n121\t        # stale head from a PRIOR prune generation and any head that does not descend from the accepted\n122\t        # one. NOTE: true SINGLE-SUCCESSOR uniqueness (rejecting a second, different owner-signed child of\n123\t        # the same parent) is NOT enforced here — that requires the pre-committed snapshot_seq cross-check\n124\t        # landing in Slice D/E; here the monotonic entry_count/base_* guards already bound a sibling.\n125\t        if head_sig_hash(head) != floor.head_sig_hash and head.prev_head_hash != floor.head_sig_hash:\n126\t            return False, (\"META-CHAIN BREAK: v2 head neither is the recorded accepted head nor descends \"\n127\t                           \"from it (stale/forked owner-signed head replay)\")\n128\t    return True, \"within durable floor\"\n129\t\n130\t\n131\t@contextmanager\n132\tdef floor_lock(path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Iterator[None]:\n133\t    \"\"\"Exclusive CROSS-PROCESS lock serializing a floor advance (and the head write that precedes it in\n134\t    checkpoint()) so the load->check->write triple is ATOMIC and last-writer-MONOTONIC, not\n135\t    last-writer-wins. Without it, two racing checkpoint() callers each read a STALE prior floor, both pass\n136\t    the downgrade guard, and the later os.replace can roll the floor BACKWARDS — opening a false-CLEAN\n137\t    window for a stale-head replay. Binds the flock to a sibling lockfile's inode (survives the atomic\n138\t    replace of floor.json). Best-effort where fcntl is absent (single-host assumption).\"\"\"\n139\t    p = Path(path) if path is not None else FLOOR_PATH\n140\t    lockp = p.parent / (p.name + \".lock\")\n141\t    fd = None\n142\t    try:\n143\t        try:\n144\t            lockp.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n145\t            fd = os.open(str(lockp), os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT, 0o600)\n146\t            if fcntl is not None:\n147\t                try:\n148\t                    fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)\n149\t                except OSError:  # pragma: no cover — lock unsupported on this fs; best-effort\n150\t                    pass\n151\t        except OSError:\n152\t            # Cannot create/open the lockfile — a read-only SIGIL_HOME, a root-owned floor.json.lock left\n153\t            # by a stray `sudo`, ENOSPC. DEGRADE to best-effort UNLOCKED rather than brick signing (the\n154\t            # `fcntl is None` path degrades the same way). A genuinely unwritable home then fails at the\n155\t            # floor `_write` and routes to checkpoint()'s LOUD branch — never the head sign itself.\n156\t            fd = None\n157\t        yield\n158\t    finally:\n159\t        if fd is not None:\n160\t            os.close(fd)\n161\t\n162\t\n163\tdef _write(floor: Floor, path: Optional[Path]) -> Floor:\n164\t    p = Path(path) if path is not None else FLOOR_PATH\n165\t    atomic_write_text(p, floor.model_dump_json(), prefix=\".floor-\")\n166\t    try:\n167\t        os.chmod(p, 0o600)\n168\t    except OSError:  # pragma: no cover — non-POSIX / unusual fs; content is non-secret, mode is best-effort\n169\t        pass\n170\t    return floor\n171\t\n172\t\n173\tdef _now_iso() -> str:\n174\t    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()\n175\t\n176\t\n177\tdef _floor_of(head: SignedChainHead) -> Floor:\n178\t    return Floor(scope=SCOPE, entry_count=head.entry_count, last_seq=head.last_seq,\n179\t                 base_seq=head.base_seq, base_count=head.base_count,\n180\t                 head_sig_hash=head_sig_hash(head), updated_ts=_now_iso())\n181\t\n182\t\n183\tdef advance_floor(head: SignedChainHead, *, path: Optional[Path] = None, _locked: bool = False) -> Floor:\n184\t    \"\"\"Advance the durable floor to a just-committed head — UPWARD-ONLY. The load->check->write is done\n185\t    under `floor_lock` and the prior floor is RE-LOADED inside the lock, so a concurrent advance that\n186\t    already wrote a higher floor is observed and this stale write is REFUSED (raises ValueError) — making\n187\t    the floor last-writer-MONOTONIC, never last-writer-wins. Refuses to lower any monotonic field or break\n188\t    the meta-chain — the exact thing the floor exists to prevent. Written atomically\n189\t    (temp->fsync->replace->dir-fsync) at 0600, only AFTER the head has committed. `_locked=True` when the\n190\t    caller (checkpoint) already holds `floor_lock` for the same critical section — do not re-acquire.\"\"\"\n191\t    p = Path(path) if path is not None else FLOOR_PATH\n192\t    if _locked:\n193\t        return _advance_locked(head, p)\n194\t    with floor_lock(p):\n195\t        return _advance_locked(head, p)\n196\t\n197\t\n198\tdef _advance_locked(head: SignedChainHead, p: Path) -> Floor:\n199\t    prior = load_floor(p)                                # RE-LOAD under the lock — sees a racing advance\n200\t    if prior is not None:\n201\t        ok, msg = check_floor(head, prior)\n202\t        if not ok:\n203\t            raise FloorDowngrade(f\"refusing to advance the durable floor DOWNWARD/off-chain: {msg}\")\n204\t    return _write(_floor_of(head), p)\n205\t\n206\t\n207\tdef reset_floor(head: SignedChainHead, *, path: Optional[Path] = None) -> Floor:\n208\t    \"\"\"DELIBERATE downward re-seed — the ONLY path that may lower the floor. For a legitimate\n209\t    `--reset` / restore, gated by the caller: `sigil floor reset` re-signs the current spine with the\n210\t    owner key (so the new floor names a fresh owner-signed head) and requires an explicit `--yes`. No\n211\t    monotonic guard — that is the whole point — but still under `floor_lock` so it cannot interleave with\n212\t    a concurrent advance and lose its write.\"\"\"\n213\t    p = Path(path) if path is not None else FLOOR_PATH\n214\t    with floor_lock(p):\n215\t        return _write(_floor_of(head), p)\n216\t"}]},"uuid":"9ee67127-7fbd-48fb-9c9a-d0f4f4135407","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:13.617Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"17e62d93-30db-4091-8a25-67806f830fcc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"e1d87c59-0918-41f9-bd34-3b4f15af9c9e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DxgGrmcf9WjPZsGf2uyQR6","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"The spine's segment MANIFEST — the sole linearization point for the retain-all segment set.\n2\t\n3\tThe spine is stored as a sequence of size-bounded, immutable **sealed** segments plus exactly one\n4\t**active** (append) segment; the manifest names them in seq order. It is deliberately UNSIGNED and\n5\tNON-LOAD-BEARING: record order, count, and every tamper decision are re-derived from the segment BYTES\n6\t(via `verify_chain`/`classify_head`), never from the manifest's convenience fields. A doctored manifest\n7\ttherefore fails CLOSED — dropping/reordering an interior segment breaks `prev_hash`/seq contiguity, a\n8\tdropped tail shortens `entries()` (the signed head's `n < entry_count` gate fires), a dropped front fails\n9\tthe genesis check. (A future signed-manifest tier would set `manifest_sig` — reserved `null` — because it\n10\twould then attest pruned ranges; retain-all needs no such trust.)\n11\t\n12\t`SpineLayout` derives the whole on-disk layout from the spine data-file path a `SpineStore` is constructed\n13\twith, so a store on a temp path is fully isolated. This module does no locking — the caller (`SpineStore`)\n14\tholds the cross-process flock while reading/writing/migrating.\n15\t\"\"\"\n16\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n17\t\n18\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n19\tfrom pathlib import PurePosixPath, Path\n20\t\n21\tfrom pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator\n22\t\n23\tfrom ..reuse.chain import _GENESIS_PREV\n24\tfrom .atomicio import atomic_write_text\n25\t\n26\tSEGMENT_STEM = \"seg-\"\n27\t_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1\n28\t\n29\t\n30\tdef segment_filename(seg_id: int, codec: str = \"none\") -> str:\n31\t    \"\"\"Zero-padded segment file name; `.gz` suffix when gzip-sealed (Slice 4).\"\"\"\n32\t    base = f\"{SEGMENT_STEM}{seg_id:08d}.jsonl\"\n33\t    return base + \".gz\" if codec == \"gzip\" else base\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tclass Segment(BaseModel):\n37\t    \"\"\"One segment in the manifest. `first_prev_hash`/`boundary_hash` are the chain-linkage the reader\n38\t    CROSS-CHECKS against the segment's real first/last records — they are convenience, not authority.\"\"\"\n39\t    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"ignore\")   # forward-compatible: tolerate fields a newer writer added\n40\t    id: int = Field(ge=0)\n41\t    file: str                                    # path RELATIVE to the spine dir, e.g. \"segments/seg-00000000.jsonl\"\n42\t    codec: str = \"none\"                          # \"none\" | \"gzip\"\n43\t    sealed: bool = False\n44\t    first_seq: int = Field(ge=0)\n45\t    last_seq: int | None = None                  # null while active\n46\t    count: int | None = None                     # null while active; CONVENIENCE ONLY — never a tamper input\n47\t    first_prev_hash: str = _GENESIS_PREV         # == the prior segment's boundary_hash (seg 0 == genesis)\n48\t    boundary_hash: str | None = None             # == entry_hash(last_seq); null while active\n49\t    bytes: int = 0\n50\t    sha256: str | None = None                    # at-rest file integrity; NOT a chain digest\n51\t\n52\t    @field_validator(\"file\")\n53\t    @classmethod\n54\t    def _file_is_contained_relative(cls, v: str) -> str:\n55\t        \"\"\"A segment `file` must be a plain relative path under the spine dir — never absolute and never\n56\t        `..`-escaping. `get()`/`iter_records()`/`tail()` open segment files WITHOUT running verify(), so a\n57\t        doctored manifest pointing `file` at `/etc/passwd` or `../../secret` would otherwise surface that\n58\t        file's bytes AS spine records. Reject it at the model boundary so the read path stays fail-closed\n59\t        even though the manifest itself is unsigned.\"\"\"\n60\t        pp = PurePosixPath(v)\n61\t        if not v or pp.is_absolute() or PurePosixPath(v.replace(\"\\\\\", \"/\")).is_absolute() or \"..\" in pp.parts:\n62\t            raise ValueError(f\"segment file must be a contained relative path, got {v!r}\")\n63\t        return v\n64\t\n65\t\n66\tclass Manifest(BaseModel):\n67\t    model_config = ConfigDict(extra=\"ignore\")\n68\t    schema_version: int = _SCHEMA_VERSION\n69\t    generation: int = Field(default=0, ge=0)     # monotonic epoch; ++ on EVERY swap — the unified change token\n70\t    scope: str = \"\"\n71\t    segments: list[Segment] = Field(default_factory=list)\n72\t    manifest_sig: str | None = None              # reserved for the deferred signed-manifest tier\n73\t\n74\t    def active(self) -> Segment | None:\n75\t        \"\"\"The single unsealed (append) segment, if any. There is at most one.\"\"\"\n76\t        act = [s for s in self.segments if not s.sealed]\n77\t        return act[-1] if act else None\n78\t\n79\t    def sealed_in_order(self) -> list[Segment]:\n80\t        return sorted((s for s in self.segments if s.sealed), key=lambda s: s.first_seq)\n81\t\n82\t    def ordered(self) -> list[Segment]:\n83\t        \"\"\"All segments in seq order (sealed by first_seq, then the active tail).\"\"\"\n84\t        out = self.sealed_in_order()\n85\t        act = self.active()\n86\t        if act is not None:\n87\t            out.append(act)\n88\t        return out\n89\t\n90\t\n91\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n92\tclass SpineLayout:\n93\t    \"\"\"The on-disk layout derived from a spine data-file path (…/spine.jsonl). Every artifact is\n94\t    NAMESPACED BY THE DATA FILE'S STEM (`spine.manifest.json`, `spine.lock`, `spine.segments/`) so two\n95\t    stores whose data files merely SHARE a directory — e.g. the `tempfile.mktemp()` idiom that drops files\n96\t    straight into /tmp — never collide on a manifest/lock/segments set. Without this, one migrated store's\n97\t    `manifest.json` would be read by every other store in that directory and silently steer it onto the\n98\t    wrong segment (a real cross-store contamination the review caught).\"\"\"\n99\t    data_path: Path            # the legacy/identity path (…/spine.jsonl) — the stable lock/identity token\n100\t    spine_dir: Path\n101\t    segments_dir: Path\n102\t    manifest_path: Path\n103\t    lockfile_path: Path\n104\t    trash_dir: Path\n105\t\n106\t    @classmethod\n107\t    def for_path(cls, data_path: Path | str) -> \"SpineLayout\":\n108\t        p = Path(data_path)\n109\t        d = p.parent\n110\t        ns = p.stem or p.name       # \"spine.jsonl\" -> \"spine\"; namespaces every derived artifact\n111\t        return cls(\n112\t            data_path=p, spine_dir=d, segments_dir=d / f\"{ns}.segments\",\n113\t            manifest_path=d / f\"{ns}.manifest.json\", lockfile_path=d / f\"{ns}.lock\", trash_dir=d / f\"{ns}.trash\",\n114\t        )\n115\t\n116\t    def seg_path(self, seg: Segment) -> Path:\n117\t        \"\"\"Absolute path of a segment's file (its `file` is stored relative to the spine dir). The\n118\t        `Segment.file` validator guarantees it is a plain relative path that cannot escape spine_dir.\"\"\"\n119\t        return self.spine_dir / seg.file\n120\t\n121\t    def segment_rel(self, seg_id: int, codec: str = \"none\") -> str:\n122\t        \"\"\"The manifest `file` value (relative to spine_dir) for a segment id — under the namespaced\n123\t        segments dir, e.g. 'spine.segments/seg-00000000.jsonl'.\"\"\"\n124\t        return f\"{self.segments_dir.name}/{segment_filename(seg_id, codec)}\"\n125\t\n126\t\n127\tdef read_manifest(layout: SpineLayout) -> Manifest | None:\n128\t    \"\"\"Load the manifest, or None if absent. A present-but-unparseable manifest RAISES (fail closed) —\n129\t    the atomic write path guarantees it is never torn, so a parse failure means genuine corruption, not a\n130\t    partial write, and must never be silently treated as 'no manifest' (which would strand the segments).\"\"\"\n131\t    mp = layout.manifest_path\n132\t    if not mp.exists():\n133\t        return None\n134\t    return Manifest.model_validate_json(mp.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n135\t\n136\t\n137\tdef write_manifest(layout: SpineLayout, manifest: Manifest) -> None:\n138\t    \"\"\"Atomically publish the manifest (temp→fsync→os.replace→dir-fsync). THE cutover commit instant.\"\"\"\n139\t    layout.manifest_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n140\t    atomic_write_text(layout.manifest_path, manifest.model_dump_json(), prefix=\".manifest-\")\n141\t\n142\t\n143\tdef initial_manifest(scope: str, *, active_file: str, first_seq: int = 0,\n144\t                     first_prev_hash: str = _GENESIS_PREV) -> Manifest:\n145\t    \"\"\"A single-active-segment manifest (generation 0) — the shape produced by a fresh store or a\n146\t    migration of an existing single-file spine.\"\"\"\n147\t    return Manifest(\n148\t        generation=0, scope=scope,\n149\t        segments=[Segment(id=0, file=active_file, codec=\"none\", sealed=False,\n150\t                          first_seq=first_seq, first_prev_hash=first_prev_hash)],\n151\t    )\n152\t"}]},"uuid":"c95a0aa6-e605-4012-895f-84af21cd43fe","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:40:13.621Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"e1d87c59-0918-41f9-bd34-3b4f15af9c9e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

Ceiling A2 (proposes; destructive steps queue).\n3\t\n4\tPLAN → PREVIEW → APPROVE → EXECUTE → VERIFY → ROLLBACK/UNDO:\n5\t  • tier is DERIVED, never declared — each step's tool token (`fs.read`/`fs.write`/`fs.delete`/\n6\t    `shell.exec.<argv0>`) is classified by the fail-closed Rust oracle (`KernelClassifier`), and the\n7\t    approval requirement is RE-DERIVED from the hash-bound preview at execute (never a trusted field).\n8\t  • scope-gated (two rings): reads/writes must resolve inside the READ ring; a write auto-applies (A1)\n9\t    only inside the narrower AUTO-WRITE ring, else it is A2 (queued). Empty rings = deny-all.\n10\t  • PREVIEW mutates NOTHING; the approval BINDS to the previewed content hash — before executing, the\n11\t    Operator re-previews the CURRENT files and aborts on any mismatch (anti-TOCTOU).\n12\t  • the executable plan (with file contents) lives in a 0700 journal, NOT the append-only spine — no\n13\t    file bytes leak into immutable memory; the spine record carries only hashes.\n14\t  • EXECUTE journals a pre-image (bytes + mode) before each mutating step, applies atomically,\n15\t    RESTORES the original mode, and VERIFIES by re-reading the post-image hash.\n16\t  • ROLLBACK restores every applied step in reverse and is HONEST about a restore that failed or an\n17\t    irreversible `shell` that already ran. UNDO is hash-bound (won't clobber newer work), scope-re-\n18\t    resolved, and single-shot (a second undo is refused).\"\"\"\n19\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n20\t\n21\timport difflib\n22\timport json\n23\timport os\n24\timport shutil\n25\timport subprocess\n26\timport tempfile\n27\tfrom dataclasses import asdict, dataclass, field\n28\tfrom pathlib import Path\n29\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n30\t\n31\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n32\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex\n33\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n34\tfrom .operator_scope import OperatorScope\n35\t\n36\t_OP_HOME = SIGIL_HOME / \"operator\"\n37\t_JOURNAL = _OP_HOME / \"journal\"          # per-execute pre-images\n38\t_PLANS = _OP_HOME / \"plans\"              # executable plans (with content) — off the spine\n39\t\n40\t\n41\tdef _hash_bytes(b: bytes) -> str:\n42\t    return sha256_hex(b)\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tdef _secure(d: Path) -> None:\n46\t    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n47\t    try:\n48\t        os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n49\t    except OSError:\n50\t        pass\n51\t\n52\t\n53\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n54\tclass Step:\n55\t    op: str                                   # read | write | delete | shell\n56\t    path: Optional[str] = None\n57\t    content: Optional[str] = None\n58\t    argv: Optional[List[str]] = None\n59\t    cwd: Optional[str] = None\n60\t\n61\t\n62\tdef _tool_token(step: Step) -> str:\n63\t    if step.op in (\"read\", \"write\", \"delete\"):\n64\t        return f\"fs.{step.op}\"\n65\t    if step.op == \"shell\":\n66\t        return f\"shell.exec.{Path((step.argv or ['?'])[0]).name}\"   # 'exec' → A3 by the oracle\n67\t    return \"unknown.op\"                                              # → fail-closed A3\n68\t\n69\t\n70\t@dataclass\n71\tclass StepPreview:\n72\t    step: dict\n73\t    tool_token: str\n74\t    tier: str\n75\t    in_scope: bool\n76\t    reversible: bool\n77\t    detail: str                                # diff/manifest/argv — DISPLAY ONLY (never persisted)\n78\t    pre_hash: Optional[str] = None\n79\t    post_hash: Optional[str] = None\n80\t\n81\t\n82\t@dataclass\n83\tclass PreviewReport:\n84\t    valid: bool\n85\t    plan_hash: str\n86\t    plan_tier: str\n87\t    steps: List[StepPreview] = field(default_factory=list)\n88\t    reason: str = \"\"\n89\t    plan_seq: Optional[int] = None\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tclass Operator(Agent):\n93\t    name = \"OPERATOR\"\n94\t    mandate = \"open files + run commands on request; tiered, previewed, transactional, reversible\"\n95\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n96\t\n97\t    def __init__(self, store=None, *, scope: Optional[OperatorScope] = None, classifier=None,\n98\t                 trusted_pubkey=None):\n99\t        super().__init__(store)\n100\t        self.scope = scope or OperatorScope()\n101\t        self._classifier = classifier\n102\t        self._trusted_pubkey = trusted_pubkey\n103\t\n104\t    def _cls(self):\n105\t        if self._classifier is None:\n106\t            from .kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n107\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n108\t        return self._classifier\n109\t\n110\t    def _tp(self):\n111\t        if self._trusted_pubkey is None:\n112\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n113\t            self._trusted_pubkey = owner_pubkey()\n114\t        return self._trusted_pubkey\n115\t\n116\t    # --- B3: grounded read (hardlink-guarded) -----------------------------------------------------\n117\t    def read(self, path: str, question: str = \"\") -> AgentResult:\n118\t        rp = self.scope.resolve(path, \"read\")\n119\t        # BLOCK-1: a regular file with >1 hardlink cannot be confined to the ring by path (a link\n120\t        # inside the root may alias an out-of-scope inode) → refuse, fail-closed.\n121\t        if rp is not None and rp.is_file() and rp.stat().st_nlink > 1:\n122\t            rp = None\n123\t        if rp is None:\n124\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n125\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": path,\n126\t                                       \"reason\": \"path is outside the Operator read scope (or a hardlink)\"})\n127\t            res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n128\t            res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED read {path}: outside scope / hardlink (logged, not read)\")\n129\t            return res\n130\t        try:\n131\t            data = rp.read_bytes()\n132\t        except OSError as e:\n133\t            res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n134\t         \"content\": data.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\")[:20000],   # verbatim bytes — authoritative\n141\t            \"grounded\": True,\n142\t        }, Tier.A0)])\n143\t\n144\t    # --- B4: preview (no mutation; content journaled off-spine) -----------------------------------\n145\t    def _preview_step(self, step: Step) -> StepPreview:\n146\t        token = _tool_token(step)\n147\t        tier = self._cls().classify(token)\n148\t        in_scope, reversible, detail = True, False, \"\"\n149\t        pre_hash = post_hash = None\n150\t        if step.op == \"shell\":\n151\t            in_scope = self.scope.in_read(step.cwd) if step.cwd else True\n152\t            detail = \"argv: \" + \" \".join(step.argv or []) + (f\"  (cwd {step.cwd})\" if step.cwd else \"\")\n153\t        else:\n154\t            rp = self.scope.resolve(step.path or \"\", \"read\")   # both reads AND writes must be in the READ ring\n155\t            in_scope = rp is not None\n156\t            old = None\n157\t            if rp is not None and rp.exists():\n158\t                try:\n159\t                    old = rp.read_bytes(); pre_hash = _hash_bytes(old)\n160\t                except OSError:\n161\t                    old = None\n162\t            if step.op == \"read\":\n163\t                detail, reversible = f\"read {step.path}\", True\n164\t            elif step.op == \"write\":\n165\t                new = (step.content or \"\").encode(\"utf-8\")\n166\t                post_hash = _hash_bytes(new)\n167\t                oldt = (old or b\"\").decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\")\n168\t                detail = \"\".join(difflib.unified_diff(\n169\t                    oldt.splitlines(keepends=True), (step.content or \"\").splitlines(keepends=True),\n170\t                    fromfile=f\"a/{step.path}\", tofile=f\"b/{step.path}\")) or \"(no textual change)\"\n171\t                reversible = True\n172\t            elif step.op == \"delete\":\n173\t                detail = f\"DELETE {step.path} (sha256 {pre_hash or 'missing'})\"\n174\t                reversible = old is not None\n175\t        return StepPreview(step=asdict(step), tool_token=token, _plan_hash(previews: List[StepPreview]) -> str:\n180\t        basis = [{\"step\": p.step, \"tool_token\": p.tool_token, \"pre_hash\": p.pre_hash,\n181\t                  \"post_hash\": p.post_hash} for p in previews]\n182\t        m = canonical_json(basis)\n183\t        return sha256_hex(m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode())\n184\t\n185\t    def _effective_tier(self, p: StepPreview) -> Tier:\n186\t        t = {\"A0\": Tier.A0, \"A1\": Tier.A1, \"A2\": Tier.A2, \"A3\": Tier.A3}[p.tier]\n187\t        if p.step[\"op\"] in (\"write\", \"delete\"):\n188\t            if not p.reversible:\n189\t                t = max(t, Tier.A3)                                  # irreversible → explicit\n190\t            elif not self.scope.in_auto_write(p.step[\"path\"] or \"\"):\n191\t                t = max(t, Tier.A2)                                  # in read ring, not auto-write → queue\n192\t        return t\n193\t\n194\t    def preview(self, steps: List[Step], *, subject: str = \"\") -> tuple[PreviewReport, AgentResult]:\n195\t        previews = [self._preview_step(s) for s in steps]\n196\t        if any(not p.in_scope for p in previews):\n197\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n198\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"subject\": subject,\n199\t                                       \"reason\": \"plan has out-of-scope step(s)\"})\n200\t            res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n201\t            res.notes.append(\"REFUSED plan: out-of-scope step(s) — nothing recorded as executable\")\n202\t            return PreviewReport(False, \"\", \"A3\", previews, \"refused: out-of-scope\"), res\n203\t        plan_hash = self._plan_hash(previews)\n204\t        plan_tier = max((self._effective_tier(p) for p in previews), default=Tier.A0)\n205\t        # executable plan (WITH content) → 0700 journal, keyed by hash; NEVER the append-only spine\n206\t        _secure(_OP_HOME); _secure(_PLANS)\n207\t        (_PLANS / f\"{plan_hash}.json\").write_text(\n208\t            json.dumps({\"plan_hash\": plan_hash, \"steps\": [asdict(s) for s in steps]}), self._dispatch([Proposal(\"operation\", {\n214\t            \"signal\": \"operator.plan\", \"subject\": subject or \"operator plan\",\n215\t            \"summary\": f\"plan: {len(steps)} step(s), tier {plan_tier.label()}, hash {plan_hash[:12]}\",\n216\t            \"plan_hash\": plan_hash, \"plan_tier\": plan_tier.label(), \"plan_journal\": str(_PLANS / f\"{plan_hash}.json\"),\n217\t            \"steps_redacted\": redacted,\n218\t            \"previews\": [{\"op\": p.step[\"op\"], \"path\": p.step.get(\"path\"), \"tool_token\": p.tool_token,\n219\t                          \"tier\": p.tier, \"reversible\": p.reversible, \"pre_hash\": p.pre_hash,\n220\t                          \"post_hash\": p.post_hash} for p in previews],\n221\t        }, plan_tier)])\n222\t        rep = PreviewReport(True, plan_hash, plan_tier.label(), previews, \"previewed (no mutation)\")\n223\t        rep.plan_seq = res.applied[0] if res.applied else (res.queued[0][\"seq\"] if res.queued else None)\n224\t        return rep, res\n225\t\n226\t    # --- B5: execute --------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n227\t    def _approved(self, plan_seq: int) ->
