Each agent has a MANDATE and an AUTONOMY\n2\tCEILING (A0–A3); everything it does is a `Proposal` carrying a tier. Proposals at or below the\n3\tauto bar (A1) and at or below the agent's ceiling are AUTO-APPLIED (written to the spine);\n4\tanything higher is QUEUED as a pending record a human must approve — never auto-executed.\n5\t\n6\tThe doctrine is enforced STRUCTURALLY, not by discipline: an agent literally cannot take an\n7\texternal-effect action, because no agent has a send/deploy/spend code path. ENVOY, in\n8\tparticular, drafts only — its ceiling is A2 and there is no method that transmits. Agent output\n9\tis provenance-linked spine records, so a brief or a draft is auditable back to what produced it.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n13\tfrom enum import IntEnum\n14\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, List, Optional\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n17\t\n18\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n19\t    from ..governor.budget import Usage\n20\t\n21\tAGENTS_SOURCE = \"agent\"\n22\t\n23\t\n24\tclass Tier(IntEnum):\n25\t    \"\"\"Mirror of the WARDEN tiers (SIGIL §5), ordered so `<=` compares autonomy.\"\"\"\n26\t    A0 = 0   # observe / answer\n27\t    A1 = 1   # reversible internal act (write a report/brief/event) — the AUTO bar\n28\t    A2 = 2   # external-visible / semi-reversible (send, calendar write) — QUEUED\n29\t    A3 = 3   # destructive / financial / security — explicit, never auto\n30\t\n31\t    def label(self) -> str:\n32\t        return self.name\n33\t\n34\t\n35\tAUTO_BAR = Tier.A1  # A0/A1 auto-apply; A2/A3 queue for human approval\n36\t\n37\t\n38\t@dataclass\n39\tclass Proposal:\n40\t    \"\"\"One thing an agent wants to do: emit a record (auto) or take a gated action (queued).\"\"\"\n41\t    kind: str                       # spine record kind (event/finding/brief/interaction/draft/...)\n42\t    payload: dict\n43\t    tier: Tier = Tier.A1\n44\t    parent_id: Optional[int] = None\n45\t    supersedes_id: Optional[int] = None   # this record supersedes an earlier one (e.g. a resolution)\n46\t    usage: Optional[\"Usage\"] = None       # provider token/cost for this action (metered on the spine);\n47\t    # OPTIONAL — when None nothing is stamped and the record is byte-identical to the pre-metering path\n48\t\n49\t\n50\t@dataclass\n51\tclass AgentResult:\n52\t    agent: str\n53\t    applied: List[int] = field(default_factory=list)     # seqs written (auto)\n54\t    queued: List[dict] = field(default_factory=list)      # proposals held for approval\n55\t    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n56\t\n57\t\n58\tclass Agent:\n59\t    \"\"\"Base agent. Subclasses implement `run()`; the base routes every Proposal through the GOVERNOR\n60\t    (SIGIL §5: kill switch, budgets, promotion — Phase 6) and records the outcome on the spine.\n61\t    Backward compatible: with no kill/cap/promotion the governor reproduces the original gate.\"\"\"\n62\t    name: str = \"AGENT\"\n63\t    mandate: str = \"\"\n64\t    ceiling: Tier = Tier.A1\n65\t\n66\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, governor=None):\n67\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n68\t        if governor is None:\n69\t            from ..governor import Governor\n70\t            governor = Governor(self.store)\n71\t        self.governor = governor\n72\t\n73\t    def run(self) -> AgentResult:\n74\t        raise NotImplementedError\n75\t\n76\t    def _dispatch(self, proposals: List[Proposal]) -> AgentResult:\n77\t        from ..governor import Outcome\n78\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n79\t        prices = None\n80\t        for p in proposals:\n81\t            effective = max(p.tier, Tier.A0)\n82\t            # promotion scope = the RECORD KIND (the real action written), not a self-asserted label:\n83\t            # a promotion is \"this agent may auto-approve A2 records of this kind\" (red-pen RP-3).\n84\t            decision = self.governor.decide(agent=self.name, tier=effective,\n85\t                                            ceiling=self.ceiling, scope=p.kind)\n86\t            common = {**p.payload, \"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n87\t                      \"governor\": decision.reason}\n88\t            # Metering seam (opt-in): a provider call may attach per-action token/cost usage. Stamp it\n89\t            # onto the TAKEN record's payload so the spine stays the sole ledger (BudgetLedger derives\n90\t            # token/cost from exactly this datum). Frozen once here; a denied action gets a refusal\n91\t            # record with NO usage below → it consumes no budget. No usage supplied → nothing stamped,\n92\t            # the record is byte-identical to the pre-metering path.\n93\t            if p.usage is not None:\n94\t                if prices is None:\n95\t                    from ..governor.budget import load_prices\n96\t                    prices = load_prices()\n97\t                common[\"usage\"] = p.usage.to_payload(prices)\n98\t            if decision.outcome == Outcome.AUTO:\n99\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n100\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"auto\"},\n101\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n102\t                res.applied.append(seq)\n103\t            elif decision.outcome == Outcome.QUEUE:\n104\t                seq = self.store.append(kind=p.kind, source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n105\t                                        payload={**common, \"decision\": \"queued\", \"status\": \"awaiting-approval\"},\n106\t                                        parent_id=p.parent_id, supersedes_id=p.supersedes_id)\n107\t                res.queued.append({\"seq\": seq, \"kind\": p.kind, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n108\t                                   \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\") or p.payload.get(\"to\")})\n109\t            else:  # DENY — kill switch or budget; record the refusal, take no action\n110\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=AGENTS_SOURCE, actor=self.name,\n111\t                                  payload={\"agent\": self.name, \"tier\": effective.label(),\n112\t                                           \"decision\": \"denied\", \"governor\": decision.reason,\n113\t                                           \"of_kind\": p.kind, \"subject\": p.payload.get(\"subject\")},\n114\t                                  parent_id=p.parent_id)\n115\t                res.notes.append(f\"DENIED {p.kind} ({effective.label()}): {decision.reason}\")\n116\t        return res\n117\t"}]},"uuid":"28449dd4-cb9e-4e2a-9bf6-0e8504003b63","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:45:21.586Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0faf817c-2df6-4ac8-98c8-fb380fc18f82","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"997c2030-bb84-4736-ab21-fae2248d575d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014qEzJ1jH6eH3GnUKXLpSys","type":"tool_result","content":"180\t                \"journal\": str(_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\"),\n181\t                \"fields_redacted\": [{\"name\": k, \"source\": _redact_source(step.fields[k])}\n182\t                                    for k in sorted(step.fields)],   # NAMES + refs/hashes only — NO values\n183\t                \"subject\": f\"{step.kind} on {step.service} (awaiting owner approval)\"}, tier)])\n184\t            seq = r.queued[0][\"seq\"] if r.queued else (r.applied[0] if r.applied else None)\n185\t            if seq is not None:\n186\t                queued.append(seq)\n187\t        return queued, res\n188\t\n189\t    # --- execute: verified approval → re-fetch → block-before-act → re-derive token → last-instant act\n190\t    def execute(self, step_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n191\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n192\t        rec = self.store.get(step_seq)\n193\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL:\n194\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {step_seq} is not a web-actor step\")\n195\t            return res\n196\t        token = rec.payload[\"action_token\"]\n197\t        if not action_approved(self.store, step_seq, token, self._tp()):\n198\t            res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} has no verified owner approval — NO request made\")\n199\t            return res\n200\t        # SINGLE-SHOT: one approval authorises exactly ONE action. A step that already produced an applied\n201\t        # record is refused (the append-only approval never expires, so without this guard one signature\n202\t        # would authorise unbounded repeats). Mirrors the Operator's single-shot undo (BLOCK-6).\n203\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=step_seq):\n204\t            if (r.payload.get(\"signal\") == ACTION_SIGNAL and r.payload.get(\"status\") == \"applied\"\n205\t                    and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\") == step_seq):\n206\t                res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} was already executed (seq {r.seq}) — refusing a replay (per-action)\")\n207\t                return res\n208\t        try:                                                 # executable step lives OFF the spine (0700)\n209\t            j = json.loads(Path(rec.payload[\"journal\"]).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n210\t            service, kind, url, fields = j[\"service\"], j[\"kind\"], j[\"url\"], j[\"fields\"]\n211\t        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n212\t            res.notes.append(\"step journal missing/unreadable — cannot execute\")\n213\t            return res\n214\t        if not self.scope.origin_allowed(url):               # re-check scope at execute (belt-and-suspenders)\n215\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n216\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq, \"requested\": url,\n217\t                                       \"reason\": \"the step URL is not in the owner's ActorScope at execute — aborted\"})\n218\t            res.notes.append(f\"ABORTED: {url} left the ActorScope — no action\")\n219\t            return res\n220\t        pv = self.engine.fetch(url)                          # re-fetch (anti-TOCTOU + block-before-act)\n221\t        block = detect_block(pv.status, pv.html)\n222\t        if block:\n223\t            self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n224\t                              payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": url,\n225\t                                       \"reason\": block, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n226\t                                       \"summary\": f\"{url} blocked at execute ({block}) — surfaced, no action\"})\n227\t            res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED at execute {url}: {block} — surfaced, no action\")\n228\t            return res\n229\t        if not pv.ok:                                        # unreachable/non-2xx at execute → never POST to a dead page\n230\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n231\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq, \"requested\": url,\n232\t                                       \"reason\": f\"page not retrievable at execute (status {pv.status}) — aborted\"})\n233\t            res.notes.append(f\"ABORTED: {url} not retrievable at execute (status {pv.status}) — no action\")\n234\t            return res\n235\t        # RE-DERIVE the token from the CURRENT url + page hash + vault version + journaled fields; a changed\n236\t        # URL OR page OR a credential rotation OR a journal edit → mismatch → abort (never trust a field).\n237\t        fresh = action_token(service, kind, url, sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")),\n238\t                             self._field_binding(service, fields))\n239\t        if fresh != token:\n240\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n241\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq,\n242\t                                       \"reason\": \"url/page/credential/step changed since approval (token mismatch) — aborted\"})\n243\t            res.notes.append(\"ABORTED: the world changed since approval (token mismatch) — no action\")\n244\t            return res\n245\t        # RE-CHECK the creation cap at execute (against committed applied records) so a batch previewed\n246\t        # before any execute cannot outrun cap=1 — the preview check alone counts zero applied.\n247\t        if kind == \"account.create\" and not self.scope.creation_allowed(self.store, service, url):\n248\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n249\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq, \"service\": service,\n250\t                                       \"reason\": \"per-service creation cap reached at execute (mass creation out of doctrine)\"})\n251\t            res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED account.create {service} at execute: creation cap\")\n252\t            return res\n253\t        resolved = self._resolve(service, fields)            # credentials → locals ONLY (never the spine)\n254\t        out = self.engine.act({\"url\": url, \"kind\": kind}, resolved,\n255\t                              pinned_ip=getattr(pv, \"resolved_ip\", \"\") or None)   # bind the POST to the vetted IP\n256\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n257\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"status\": \"applied\", \"target_seq\": step_seq,\n258\t                                         \"service\": service, \"step_kind\": kind, \"url\": url, \"action_token\": token,\n259\t                                         \"result_ok\": bool(out.get(\"ok\")), \"tier\": \"A1\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n260\t                                         \"summary\": f\"{kind} on {service} executed (ok={out.get('ok')})\"})\n261\t        res.applied.append(seq)\n262\t        res.notes.append(f\"executed {kind} on {service} (ok={out.get('ok')})\")\n263\t        return res\n264\t\n265\t    def _resolve(self, service: str, fields: dict) -> dict:\n266\t        \"\"\"Resolve field references to VALUES at the last instant — into a local dict, never the spine.\"\"\"\n267\t        out: dict = {}\n268\t        rec = self._vaultobj().get_record(service)\n269\t        for name, src in fields.items():"}]},"uuid":"dc86b14b-31a5-4681-b258-6684b95b569e","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:46:03.083Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"997c2030-bb84-4736-ab21-fae2248d575d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"fda87f98-c660-4f79-bb13-ed56806bbe08","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PA1KxLtpzAWjDZZhgNHBQ7","type":"tool_result","content":"119\t        Otherwise the egress is QUEUED (A2) and NOTHING is uploaded.\"\"\"\n120\t        from ..agents.kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n121\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n122\t        from .egress import EGRESS_SIGNAL, FRONTIER_TOOL, egress_approved, egress_token\n123\t        classifier = classifier or KernelClassifier()\n124\t        tp = trusted_pubkey if trusted_pubkey is not None else owner_pubkey()\n125\t        token = egress_token(frame.sha256, question)\n126\t        tier = classifier.classify(FRONTIER_TOOL)   # A2 by design (contains \"upload\") — DERIVED, not declared\n127\t\n128\t        if approved_seq is not None and egress_approved(self.store, approved_seq, token, tp):\n129\t            reading = \"\"\n130\t            try:\n131\t                reading = vision.describe(frame, question) or \"\"   # the UPLOAD — only after verified approval\n132\t            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n133\t                reading = \"\"\n134\t            res = self._emit(question, frame, reading, source_model=\"frontier\",\n135\t                             extra={\"egress_approved_seq\": approved_seq})\n136\t            res.notes.append(f\"frontier egress APPROVED (seq {approved_seq}, {tier.label()}) — uploaded + served advisory\")\n137\t            return res\n138\t\n139\t        # no verified approval → QUEUE the egress; upload NOTHING. The tier is DERIVED from the oracle\n140\t        # (A2 for \"...upload\"; A3 if the kernel is unreachable → even more gated) and A2/A3 > ceiling\n141\t        # A1 ⇒ the governor queues it. Nothing uploads on this path.\n142\t        res = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"event\", {\n143\t            \"signal\": EGRESS_SIGNAL, \"subject\": f\"frontier vision egress (frame {frame.sha256[:12]})\",\n144\t            \"summary\": \"frontier vision egress requested — upload WITHHELD pending owner approval\",\n145\t            \"egress_token\": token, \"frame_sha256\": frame.sha256, \"question\": question,\n146\t        }, tier)])\n147\t        res.notes.append(\"frontier egress QUEUED — NO image uploaded; `sigil approve <seq>` then re-run \"\n148\t                         \"`perceive --frontier --approved <seq>`\")\n149\t        return res\n150\t\n151\t    def ambient_watch(self, frames: List[Frame], *, vision: Optional[VisionModel] = None) -> tuple:\n152\t        \"\"\"Opt-in ambient loop (C6). Establish a baseline, then ESCALATE only on a PERCEPTUAL change\n153\t        (`delta.changed` — OCR-token-set divergence, so lighting jitter with the same on-screen text\n154\t        is not a change). Returns (escalation_count, [AgentResult]); emits indicator start/stop\n155\t        markers; unchanged frames produce nothing (they never leave the machine).\"\"\"\n156\t        if vision is not None and getattr(vision, \"egresses\", False):\n157\t            # ambient must NEVER auto-upload to the frontier — hard refuse (red-pen BLOCK-2).\n158\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,"}]},"uuid":"1c8a3e89-9836-4b85-8a2a-c489e373b5c3","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:49:25.930Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fda87f98-c660-4f79-bb13-ed56806bbe08","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"5a08a6d6-8c4d-4546-b076-257b092a0085","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"440dc55d-7346-4ae6-a6fa-9f9e6bac39c4","agentId":"a8b19cafd020e87a3","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FguDoZUpqPh6fv9dwzEFS4","type":"tool_result","content":"150\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {step.url}: invalid field source(s) {bad}\")\n151\t                continue\n152\t            pv = self.engine.fetch(step.url)                 # A0 dry-run GET — no mutation\n153\t            block = detect_block(pv.status, pv.html)\n154\t            if block:                                        # STOP + surface — never defeat a block\n155\t                self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n156\t                                  payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": step.url,\n157\t                                           \"reason\": block, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n158\t                                           \"summary\": f\"{step.url} is blocked ({block}) — surfaced, NOT defeated\"})\n159\t                res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED {step.url}: {block} — surfaced, not defeated (doctrine)\")\n160\t                continue\n161\t            if not pv.ok:                                    # a non-2xx / unreachable page has nothing to act on\n162\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n163\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": step.url,\n164\t                                           \"reason\": f\"page not retrievable (status {pv.status}) — nothing to act on\"})\n165\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {step.url}: page not retrievable (status {pv.status})\")\n166\t                continue\n167\t            page_hash = sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\"))\n168\t            fb = self._field_binding(step.service, step.fields)\n169\t            token = action_token(step.service, step.kind, step.url, page_hash, fb)\n170\t            _secure(_ACTOR_HOME)\n171\t            _secure(_JOURNAL)\n172\t            # executable step (WITH any literal values) → 0700 journal, keyed by token; NEVER the spine\n173\t            (_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\").write_text(json.dumps(\n174\t                {\"service\": step.service, \"kind\": step.kind, \"url\": step.url, \"fields\": step.fields}),\n175\t                encoding=\"utf-8\")\n176\t            tier = self._cls().classify(_TOOL.get(step.kind, \"unknown.op\"))   # → A3 for the mutating kinds\n177\t            r = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"event\", {\n178\t                \"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"service\": step.service, \"step_kind\": step.kind, \"url\": step.url,\n179\t                \"page_sha256\": page_hash, \"field_binding\": fb, \"action_token\": token,\n180\t                \"journal\": str(_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\"),\n181\t                \"fields_redacted\": [{\"name\": k, \"source\": _redact_source(step.fields[k])}\n182\t                                    for k in sorted(step.fields)],   # NAMES + refs/hashes only — NO values\n183\t                \"subject\": f\"{step.kind} on {step.service} (awaiting owner approval)\"}, tier)])\n184\t            seq = r.queued[0][\"seq\"] if r.queued else (r.applied[0] if r.applied else None)\n185\t            if seq is not None:\n186\t                queued.append(seq)\n187\t        return queued, res\n188\t\n189\t    # --- execute: verified approval → re-fetch → block-before-act → re-derive token → last-instant act"}]},"uuid":"140b4ae5-b948-425e-a55c-609e4890594e","timestamp":"2026-07-20T00:49:31.478Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5a08a6d6-8c4d-4546-b076-257b092a0085","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
